- **nesting selector (`&`)**: the selector that stands for the parent rule's selector in a nested rule, in native CSS and in Sass ([10 §7](../modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).
- **BEM**: Block Element Modifier, a class naming convention such as `.card__title` ([10 §7](../modules/10-css-essentials.md#7-native-nesting-versus-sass)).

## Module 11

- **accessibility**: the property that people with different abilities and in different situations can perceive, operate and understand a page ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **assistive technology (AT)**: software or hardware a person uses to reach a page, such as a screen reader, magnifier or switch ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **WCAG**: the W3C's Web Content Accessibility Guidelines, a layered standard of principles, guidelines and testable success criteria ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **success criterion (SC)**: one testable WCAG requirement, at level A, AA or AAA ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **conformance level**: A, AA or AAA, each including the levels below it ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **POUR**: WCAG's four principles, perceivable, operable, understandable, robust ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **European Accessibility Act (EAA)**: Directive (EU) 2019/882, accessibility requirements for certain products and services, applied from 28 June 2025 ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **EN 301 549**: a harmonised European standard of accessibility requirements for ICT products and services; V3.2.1 (2021-03) maps WCAG 2.1 AA for Directive 2016/2102, V4.1.1 (2026-09) WCAG 2.2 and adds Directive 2019/882 ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **harmonised standard**: a European standard adopted on a Commission request for Union harmonisation legislation (Directive (EU) 2019/882 Article 3, Regulation (EU) No 1025/2012); citation in the Official Journal gives a presumption of conformity ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **microenterprise**: in the EAA, an enterprise with fewer than 10 persons and annual turnover or balance sheet total of at most EUR 2 million; exempt for services (Article 4(5)) ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **jsdom**: a JavaScript implementation of the DOM and HTML Standards for Node.js, used by the labs; it has no accessibility tree or layout ([11](../modules/11-accessibility.md)).
- **relative luminance and contrast ratio**: the 0 to 1 brightness of a colour, and `(L1 + 0.05) / (L2 + 0.05)` over two of them ([11 §1](../modules/11-accessibility.md#1-why-accessibility-and-what-the-standards-require)).
- **accessibility tree**: the browser's tree of objects with a name, description, role and state, built from the DOM and exposed to assistive technology ([11 §2](../modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- **platform accessibility API**: the operating-system interface through which a browser hands the accessibility tree to assistive technology ([11 §2](../modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- **role**: what kind of thing an accessibility-tree node is, such as button, link or heading ([11 §2](../modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- **landmark**: a region role (banner, main, navigation, complementary, contentinfo, region) that assistive technology can jump to ([11 §2](../modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- **heading level**: the number 1 to 6 of an `h1` to `h6`, which gives the page its outline ([11 §2](../modules/11-accessibility.md#2-semantic-html-landmarks-and-headings)).
- **Baseline**: MDN's label for cross-browser availability, newly available or widely available ([11 header](../modules/11-accessibility.md)).
- **ARIA**: Accessible Rich Internet Applications, the W3C vocabulary of roles, states and properties that annotates the accessibility tree and adds no behavior ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **APG**: the ARIA Authoring Practices Guide, W3C's reference for how widgets should behave ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **state / property (ARIA)**: an `aria-*` attribute for something that changes often (`aria-expanded`) or a relationship that rarely does (`aria-labelledby`) ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **widget / composite widget**: an interactive control, and one that contains items such as a menu or tab list ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **presentational role**: `presentation` or `none`, which removes an element's own semantics while keeping its content exposed ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **name prohibited**: a role (such as `generic`) on which `aria-label` and `aria-labelledby` must not be used ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **inert**: the global attribute that makes an element and its descendants unfocusable, unclickable and absent from the accessibility tree ([11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules)).
- **focus / focusable element**: the element that receives keyboard input, and an element that can take it ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **tab order / tab stop**: the sequence Tab and Shift+Tab follow through focusable elements, and one position in it ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **roving tabindex**: a composite-widget technique where exactly one item has `tabindex="0"` and arrow keys move that stop ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **`aria-activedescendant`**: a property on a focused container naming the descendant that assistive technology should treat as active ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **focus indicator**: the visible mark of keyboard focus, styled with `:focus` or `:focus-visible` ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **skip link**: a link at the top of the page that moves focus past repeated content to the main content ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **focus trap / modal dialog**: code that keeps Tab inside a region, and a dialog that makes the rest of the page inert while open ([11 §4](../modules/11-accessibility.md#4-keyboard-focus-order-roving-tabindex-and-focus-traps)).
- **single-page application (SPA)**: an app that swaps content inside one document on navigation instead of loading a new page ([11 §5](../modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- **live region**: an element whose text changes assistive technology reports without the user moving focus ([11 §5](../modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- **politeness (`aria-live`)**: `off`, `polite` or `assertive`: whether and how urgently a live-region change is announced ([11 §5](../modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- **status message**: WCAG 4.1.3's term for a change of status the user should learn of without focus moving ([11 §5](../modules/11-accessibility.md#5-focus-management-in-spas-and-live-regions)).
- **accessible name / accessible description**: the short text (name) and optional longer text (description) the accessibility tree holds for a node ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **AccName**: the Accessible Name and Description Computation, the W3C algorithm that picks the name from `aria-labelledby`, `aria-label`, native labels, content or `title` ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **name from content**: a role's rule that its name is the text of its children, as for buttons and links ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **label in name**: WCAG 2.5.3, the accessible name must contain the visible label text ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **error summary**: a block at the top of a form listing every error with a link to its field ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **constraint validation**: the platform's built-in form checking (`required`, `validity`, `setCustomValidity`, the `invalid` event) ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **`aria-invalid`**: the ARIA state marking that a field's value does not match the expected format ([11 §6](../modules/11-accessibility.md#6-accessible-names-descriptions-and-forms)).
- **rule engine**: a program such as axe-core that checks the rendered DOM against a list of accessibility rules ([11 §7](../modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- **violation / incomplete (review item)**: a rule result that failed, versus one the engine could neither pass nor fail and leaves for a person ([11 §7](../modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- **false negative**: a real defect a tool does not report ([11 §7](../modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- **linting**: static checks of source code, here of Angular templates, before they run ([11 §7](../modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- **screen reader**: assistive technology that speaks or brailles the accessibility tree, such as NVDA, VoiceOver, TalkBack or Narrator ([11 §7](../modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).
- **manual audit**: a human pass (keyboard, zoom, screen reader) for what rules cannot decide ([11 §7](../modules/11-accessibility.md#7-testing-accessibility)).

## Module 12

- **library**: code you call; it does some work and returns control to you ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **framework**: code that calls yours; it owns the entry point, creates your objects and decides when they run ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **inversion of control**: the reversal in which the framework, not your code, drives the program and calls into it ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **batteries included**: what most applications need (router, forms, HTTP, i18n, build, testing) ships with the framework ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **opinionated**: the framework picks one way to do each thing ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **CLI (Angular)**: the command-line interface, the `ng` command ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **compiler (Angular)**: turns templates and decorators into JavaScript at build time ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **runtime (Angular)**: the `@angular/core` code that creates components and updates the DOM in the browser ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **workspace (Angular)**: the folder whose `angular.json` maps projects to targets ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **project (Angular workspace)**: one application or library in a workspace ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **target**: a named job of a project (`build`, `serve`, `test`) with a builder, options and configurations ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **builder**: the function a target runs, named `<package>:<name>` ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **configuration (build)**: a named set of options that overrides a target's base options, selected with `-c` ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **schematic**: a code generator or rewriter that edits a virtual file tree ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **collection (schematics)**: a package's set of schematics ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **migration (ng update)**: a versioned schematic that `ng update` runs to rewrite code for a new version ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **`ng-update` field**: the `package.json` field holding a package's `packageGroup` and `migrations` ([12 §2](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#2-the-cli-and-the-workspace-angularjson-projects-schematics-and-ng-update)).
- **bundle**: a file the build emits by joining many modules ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **chunk**: a separately loaded output file; each dynamic `import()` starts one ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **code splitting**: dividing the output into chunks loaded on demand ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **minification**: rewriting code into fewer characters without changing behavior ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **output hashing**: a content hash in each output file name, so only changed files get new URLs ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **budget (build)**: a size limit on part of the build output that warns or fails the build ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **baseline (budget)**: the reference size that percentage thresholds are relative to ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **esbuild**: the bundler the `application` builder uses ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **Vite**: the development server behind `ng serve`, not the production bundler ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **rolldown**: the bundler of the optional second chunk-optimization pass ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **file replacement**: a configuration entry that makes the build load one source file in place of another ([12 §4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- **`define` (build option)**: replaces a global identifier with a constant value at build time ([12 §4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- **environment file**: a TypeScript module of per-build constants under `src/environments/`, swapped by file replacements ([12 §4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- **dead-code elimination**: the minifier deleting code that can never run ([12 §4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- **runtime configuration**: settings the app reads after it starts, not compiled into the bundle ([12 §4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time)).
- **injection token**: a typed key for a value in the injector ([12 §4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#4-environments-and-configuration-build-time-versus-run-time); owned by module 16).
- **dev server**: the `ng serve` process that rebuilds in memory on save and pushes changes to the page ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **live reload**: reloading the whole page after a rebuild ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **hot module replacement (HMR)**: replacing only the changed stylesheet or component template in the running page ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **prebundling**: Vite processing third-party dependencies once at the first `ng serve` ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **development mode**: the state in which `ngDevMode` is on and `isDevMode()` returns true ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **Angular DevTools**: the Chrome and Firefox extension that inspects a running development build ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **`ng` global utilities**: debugging functions such as `ng.getComponent` published on `window.ng` in development mode ([12 §5](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#5-the-dev-loop-ng-serve-hot-module-replacement-and-angular-devtools)).
- **bootstrap**: the startup sequence that creates the platform, the environment injector, runs initializers and renders the root component ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- **platform (Angular)**: the page-wide layer and injector shared by every Angular app on the page ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- **environment injector (root)**: the application-wide injector created from `ApplicationConfig.providers`; "the root injector" ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers); owned by module 16).
- **initializer**: a function run during bootstrap whose promise or observable must settle before the root component is created ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- **host element**: the DOM element a component renders into, such as `<lab-root>` for the root component ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- **`ApplicationRef`**: the handle to a running application: its root components, injector and `destroy()` ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- **injection context**: a place where `inject()` may be called, such as an initializer run by bootstrap ([12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers); owned by module 16).
- **AOT (ahead-of-time) compilation**: compiling templates and decorators into definitions during the build, so no compiler ships ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **JIT (just-in-time) compilation**: compiling a component in the browser or test runtime when its definition is first read; needs `@angular/compiler` loaded ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **`ngc`**: the Angular compiler's command-line tool, `tsc` plus template compilation and type checking ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **template type checking**: generating TypeScript code from template expressions so TypeScript reports template errors at build time ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **`strictTemplates`**: the compiler option for strict template type checking; defaults to `true` since v22 ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **extended diagnostics**: optional compiler checks for likely template mistakes (codes `NG81xx`, plus `NG8011`), warnings by default and configurable per check ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **Ivy**: the name of Angular's current compiler and runtime design, in which each decorator compiles to a static definition on its class ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **definition (`ɵcmp`, `ɵdir`, `ɵpipe`, `ɵprov`, `ɵfac`)**: the static property the compiler adds to a component, directive, pipe or injectable class, or its factory ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **template function**: the generated function a component's template compiles to, run with a create flag and an update flag ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **instruction**: a small runtime function (`ɵɵdomElementStart`, `ɵɵtext`, `ɵɵadvance`…) that a template function calls to create or update DOM ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **create pass and update pass**: the template function's first run, which builds a view's nodes, and later runs, which compare bindings and write the changed ones ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **slot (view storage)**: a numbered position in a view's storage that holds a node or a binding's last value ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **`decls` and `vars`**: in a component definition, the number of slots the create pass fills and the number of binding slots after them ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **`consts`**: the attribute arrays a component definition's elements refer to by index ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **view**: one rendered instance of a template, with its own nodes and binding slots ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **locality (Ivy locality principle)**: a decorator compiles with knowledge of its own class and imports only, not the whole program ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **partial compilation**: the `compilationMode: "partial"` output for npm libraries, `ɵɵngDeclare*` declarations with a `minVersion` ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **linker**: the application-build step that turns partial `ɵɵngDeclare*` declarations into full definitions (OXC by default in `@angular/build`) ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **private API (`ɵ`, `ɵɵ`)**: Angular symbols prefixed `ɵ`, `ɵɵ` or `_`, which may change in any release ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **component mirror (`ComponentMirror`)**: the read-only view of a component's compiled metadata (selector, inputs, outputs, `ng-content` selectors, standalone flag) that the public `reflectComponentType` returns ([12, Exercise 12.1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#ex12-1)).
- **tokenizer**: the first step of a template compiler, which splits the source into tags and text runs before anything is compiled ([12 Exercise 12.4](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#ex12-4)).
- **peer dependency**: a package a library requires but the consumer installs, so one shared copy is used rather than a bundled one ([12 §1](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#1-what-a-framework-does-and-angulars-philosophy)).
- **OXC**: a set of JavaScript tools written in Rust; `@angular/build` uses it to run the linker by default ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **Brotli**: a compression format; the build's "Estimated transfer size" column is a Brotli estimate ([12 §3](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#3-building-the-application-builder-output-layout-and-budgets)).
- **CMS (content management system)**: a system that stores and edits content, such as markup, outside the application's code ([12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking)).
- **dependency injection (DI)**: the mechanism that constructs a class and supplies its dependencies, calling the compiled factory `ɵfac` ([12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it)).
- **SemVer (semantic versioning)**: a version-numbering contract in which breaking changes come only in major versions ([12 Q12.25](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-25)).

## Module 13

- **component**: an Angular class plus a template and optional styles, bound to every element that matches its selector ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **template**: the HTML a component renders inside its host element ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **selector**: the restricted CSS pattern (element, attribute or class, `:not()`, comma lists) that picks the elements a component is bound to ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **host element**: the DOM element that matches a component's selector; the component's view renders inside it ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **view**: the DOM a component's template renders, inside its host element ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **metadata**: the `@Component` object, data about the class that the compiler reads to build its definition ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **prefix (selector prefix)**: a short project-wide start for selectors, such as the CLI default `app-`; `ng` is reserved for Angular ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **template expression**: the restricted JavaScript inside `{{ }}` or a binding's quotes: reads, calls of component members and operators, no side effects, declarations or globals ([13 §2](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- **interpolation**: `{{ expression }}`, a text binding that converts the value to a string ([13 §2](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- **side effect**: a change to anything outside the expression being evaluated, such as assignment or `++`; template expressions may not have one ([13 §2](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- **tagged template**: a template literal preceded by a function (`` tag`text` ``) that receives its text parts ([13 §2](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#2-interpolation-and-template-expressions-what-is-allowed)).
- **optional chaining (`?.`)**: access that stops at a `null` or `undefined` left side; in Angular templates the result is `undefined` since v22.0, `null` before ([13 §3](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)).
- **non-null assertion (`x!`)**: a claim to the type checker that a value is not `null` or `undefined`; erased at compile time, so it changes no run-time behavior ([13 §3](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)).
- **escape hatch**: a construct that bypasses a check, such as `$any(x)`, which switches type checking off for one template expression ([13 §3](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)).
- **narrowing**: the type checker learning that a value is present inside a check such as `@if (user(); as u)` ([13 §3](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#3-safe-navigation-the-non-null-assertion-and-any)).
- **binding target**: the thing a binding writes to: a DOM property, an HTML attribute, a class, a style declaration or a component input ([13 §4](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- **DOM property**: a field on an element's JavaScript object, such as `title` or `value`; `[title]` writes it ([13 §4](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- **HTML attribute**: the named text in an element's markup; `[attr.title]` writes it with `setAttribute` and removes it on `null` ([13 §4](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- **classList**: an element's live set of class names, which class bindings add to and remove from ([13 §4](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#4-property-attribute-class-and-style-bindings)).
- **event binding**: `(event)="statement"`, a subscription to a DOM event or a component output that runs the statement each time it fires ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **`$event`**: the name for the native event, or the emitted value of an output, inside an event binding's statement ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **key modifier**: `alt`, `control`, `meta` or `shift` written before the key in a key filter such as `(keydown.shift.enter)`; the set must match the pressed modifiers exactly ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **global event target**: a `window:`, `document:` or `body:` prefix that makes a `host` listener attach to that object instead of the element ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **two-way binding**: `[(x)]="y"`, syntax for the property binding `[x]` plus the listener `(xChange)` that assigns the emitted value back to `y` ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **desugaring**: rewriting convenience syntax into the simpler constructs it stands for, as `[(x)]` becomes `[x]` and `(xChange)` ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **banana-in-a-box**: the nickname for `[()]`, parentheses inside brackets; `([x])` is the common mistake ([13 §5](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#5-event-binding-and-two-way-binding)).
- **template reference variable**: `#name` on an element, a name for a DOM element, a component or directive instance or a `TemplateRef`, readable in the same view and in nested ones ([13 §6](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- **scope (template)**: the region of a template where a name can be read; each block opens a nested scope that reads outer names, never the reverse ([13 §6](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- **embedded view**: the template fragment an `@if`, `@for` block or `<ng-template>` creates, with its own scope ([13 §6](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- **`exportAs`**: the name under which a directive makes its instance available to a reference, as in `#t="labTally"` ([13 §6](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- **`TemplateRef`**: a handle on the content of an `<ng-template>`; a reference on that element holds one ([13 §6](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- **`@let`**: `@let name = expression;`, a read-only template variable that Angular keeps current; [Stable since v19.0] ([13 §6](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#6-template-reference-variables-and-let)).
- **host binding**: a binding declared in a component's `host` metadata (or with `@HostBinding`) that writes to the component's own element, not to its template ([13 §7](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- **host listener**: an event listener declared in `host` metadata (or with `@HostListener`) on the component's own element or on a global target ([13 §7](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- **binding collision**: the parent's template and the component's `host` binding the same property, attribute, class or style on one element; the winner depends on static versus dynamic and, for class and style, on the template ([13 §7](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- **`HostAttributeToken`**: an injection token that reads a static attribute of the host element with `inject()`; it throws when the attribute is missing unless the injection is optional ([13 §7](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- **legacy (API)**: still public and supported, but documented as kept for backwards compatibility, without a deprecation tag; `@HostBinding` and `@HostListener` ([13 §7](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener)).
- **component style**: CSS declared in a component's `styles`, `styleUrl` or a `<style>` element in its template; by default it reaches only that component's template ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- **global style**: a stylesheet listed in `angular.json` `styles`; its rules are not rewritten and reach every element ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- **CSS custom property**: a `--name` value that inherits down the DOM and is read with `var()`, so it crosses component boundaries ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind); owner [10 §6](../modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- **external style**: a `<link>` or `@import` reference in a component; Angular does not scope it ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- **`APP_ID`**: the injection token holding the application's id (`ng` unless set), used in generated attribute names and as the default CSS-variable namespace ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- **Tailwind CSS**: a build-time utility-class CSS framework; in Angular its stylesheet is a global one ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- **PostCSS**: a tool that transforms CSS with plugins; Tailwind's Angular setup uses its plugin ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind)).
- **design token**: a named design value, such as a colour, usually held in a custom property ([13 §8](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#8-styling-strategies-component-styles-global-styles-css-variables-and-tailwind); owner [10 §6](../modules/10-css-essentials.md#6-custom-properties-theming-and-color-scheme)).
- **view encapsulation**: how a component's CSS is kept from affecting other elements: Emulated (compiler rewrite), ShadowDom (browser shadow root) or None ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- **emulated encapsulation**: Angular's default mode: every selector is rewritten to also need an attribute that only this component's elements carry ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- **`_ngcontent` / `_nghost` attributes**: the generated attributes of emulated encapsulation, `_ngcontent-<id>` on template elements and `_nghost-<id>` on the host, sharing one suffix per component type ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- **`::ng-deep`**: Angular's custom pseudo-class that stops the selector rewrite from its position on; discouraged by the Angular team ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- **`ShadowCss`**: the private compiler class that rewrites a component's CSS for emulated encapsulation ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- **specificity** (as used in 13): the weight that decides which of two matching rules wins; owner [10 §2](../modules/10-css-essentials.md#2-the-cascade-origins-specificity-and-layers) ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).
- **jsdom** (as used in 13): a browser DOM implemented in JavaScript for Node; the specs of 13 run in it, and it resolves no `var()` ([13](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md)).
- **Vitest**: the test runner that `ng test` uses ([13](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md)).
- **`ngc`**: the Angular compiler's command line, used in 13 to observe compiler output and diagnostics ([13](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md)).
- **OnPush** (as defined in 13): a component is re-checked only when an input, an event or a signal it reads changes; the default since v22.0, owner [21](../modules/21-change-detection.md) ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **ARIA**: the attributes that tell assistive technology a role and state; owner [11](../modules/11-accessibility.md) ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **DI (dependency injection)**: Angular's way of giving a class the objects it asks for; owner [16](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md) ([13 §1](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#1-component-anatomy-selector-template-styles-imports-and-the-v22-file-convention)).
- **TestBed**: Angular's test harness, which creates a component in a test ([13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css)).

## Module 14

- **directive**: behavior attached to an existing element by a selector, with no view of its own; a component is a directive that owns a view ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **attribute directive**: a directive that changes how its element looks or behaves; the structural kind adds and removes elements ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **alias (input or output)**: the public name an input or output has in templates, set with `alias`, which can differ from the class member ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **`ElementRef`**: a wrapper whose `nativeElement` holds the host's DOM node; its typings carry an XSS `@security` note ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **`Renderer2`**: Angular's abstract class for writing to elements; per its typings it gives no extra security over direct DOM access ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **comment node**: a DOM node that holds text and renders nothing; `nativeElement` is one on `<ng-template>` and `<ng-container>` ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **XSS (cross-site scripting)**: an attack that runs an attacker's script in your page; owner [33](../modules/33-security.md) ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **host binding** (as used in 14): a `host` metadata binding on a directive's own element, the declarative channel to it; owner [13 §7](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#7-the-host-element-host-metadata-hostbinding-and-hostlistener) ([14 §1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#1-directives-and-attribute-directives-host-inputs-elementref-and-renderer2)).
- **jsdom** (as used in 14): a browser DOM implemented in JavaScript for Node; the specs of 14 run in it ([14](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md)).
- **`ngc`** (as used in 14): the Angular compiler's command line, used to observe compiler output and diagnostics ([14](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md)).
- **structural directive**: a directive on an `<ng-template>` that decides when, and how often, the template is stamped out; `*` is shorthand that wraps an element in the `<ng-template>` ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **`TemplateRef`**: the stencil: a compiled `<ng-template>` that renders nothing until an embedded view is created from it ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **`ViewContainerRef`**: the place on the page, anchored by a comment node, where embedded views are created; their root elements become its siblings ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **embedded view**: a view created from a template, with its own bindings and lifecycle ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **microsyntax**: the mini-language inside `*dir="…"` (`let`, `as`, keys such as `of`) that the compiler expands into `<ng-template>` inputs and `let-` variables ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **template context**: the object given to `createEmbeddedView`; `let-name="key"` reads a key and a bare `let` reads `$implicit` ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **`$implicit`**: the context key that a bare `let item` reads ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **`<ng-container>`**: a grouping element that renders nothing and leaves a comment node; the wrapper for a second structural directive ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **type assertion function**: a function whose return type `x is T` tells TypeScript that `x` is a `T` afterwards; the form of `ngTemplateContextGuard` and `ngTemplateGuard_input` ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **template type checker (as used in 14)**: the part of the compiler that type-checks template expressions; a directive's static guards feed it; owner [12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking) ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **`effect` (as used in 14)**: a function Angular re-runs when a signal it read changes; owner [17 §4](../modules/17-signals.md#4-effects) ([14 §2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#2-structural-directives-ng-template-templateref-viewcontainerref-and-the-microsyntax)).
- **host directive**: a directive that a class applies to its own host element through `hostDirectives`; its inputs and outputs are hidden unless listed ([14 §3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- **composition**: building a class's behavior from parts such as host directives instead of inheriting it ([14 §3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- **`aria-busy` (as used in 14)**: the ARIA state that marks an element as being updated, so assistive technology may wait; owner [11 §3](../modules/11-accessibility.md#3-aria-roles-states-properties-and-its-rules) ([14 §3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- **exposure (host directive inputs and outputs)**: listing a host directive's input or output in `inputs`/`outputs` so templates can bind it, optionally under an alias ([14 §3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- **de-duplication (host directives)**: keeping one instance of a directive reached more than once; a template match wins and repeated host-directive matches merge [Changed in v22.0] ([14 §3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- **diamond problem**: two paths reaching one shared part, so which copy applies is unclear in multiple inheritance ([14 §3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#3-host-directives-the-composition-api)).
- **built-in directive**: an ordinary directive shipped in `@angular/common` (`NgClass`, `NgStyle`, the outlets, and the deprecated `NgIf`, `NgForOf`, `NgSwitch`); nothing in it is privileged ([14 §4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- **key-value differ**: Angular's helper that compares an object's keys and values with its previous copy; `NgStyle` uses it ([14 §4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- **outlet**: a directive that renders something it is given, a template (`NgTemplateOutlet`) or a component class (`NgComponentOutlet`) ([14 §4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- **dynamic component**: a component whose class is chosen at run time, rendered through `NgComponentOutlet` ([14 §4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- **environment injector** (as used in 14): the injector of the application scope, passable to `NgComponentOutlet`; owner [16](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md) ([14 §4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#4-built-in-directives-ngclass-ngstyle-the-outlets-and-what-control-flow-replaced)).
- **pipe**: a function the template calls with `value | name: arg`, with a cache of one result per place it is used ([14 §5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- **pure pipe**: a pipe that calls `transform` again only when an argument differs by `Object.is` from the last call; the default ([14 §5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- **impure pipe**: a pipe declared `pure: false`, called on every check of its view ([14 §5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- **`Object.is`**: JavaScript's "same value" test; for objects it means the same reference; owner [01 §6](../modules/01-js-values-types-coercion.md#6-equality) ([14 §5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- **view slot**: a cell in a view's storage reserved for one binding, where a pipe usage keeps its last arguments and result; owner [12 §8](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-and-what-the-runtime-does-with-it) ([14 §5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- **node injector**: the injector tied to a template node, through which `ɵɵpipe` creates a pipe instance; owner [16](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md) ([14 §5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#5-pipes-pure-and-impure-custom-pipes-injection-and-imports)).
- **locale**: a language with an optional region (`en-GB`) whose formatting tables the pipes read; an unregistered language other than `en` is an error ([14 §6](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- **`LOCALE_ID`**: the injection token holding the locale the pipes use, `en-US` by default; owner [34](../modules/34-i18n.md) ([14 §6](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- **formatting pipe**: a pipe (`date`, `currency`, `number`, `percent`, `i18nPlural`) that turns a value into text using locale data ([14 §6](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- **comparator**: a function of two entries that decides their order, the optional argument of `keyvalue` ([14 §6](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- **plural category**: the locale's label for a number (`one`, `few`, `other`…), which `i18nPlural` tries after an exact key such as `=0` ([14 §6](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- **CLDR**: the project the typings name as the source of currency data; named only on that basis ([14 §6](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#6-built-in-pipes-and-locale-data)).
- **memoization**: remembering the last result of a function so a repeat call with the same input skips the work ([14 §7](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- **derived state**: a value computed from other state, such as a total from a list ([14 §7](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- **dependency tracking**: a `computed` recording which signals it read so it re-runs only when one changed; owner [17](../modules/17-signals.md) ([14 §7](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- **view check**: Angular re-evaluating a template's bindings; owner [21](../modules/21-change-detection.md) ([14 §7](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- **OnPush (as used in 14)**: a component setting under which the component is re-checked only when an input, an event inside it or a signal it reads changes; the default since v22.0 [Changed in v22.0]; owner [21](../modules/21-change-detection.md) ([14 §7](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#7-pipe-computed-or-method-call-in-a-template)).
- **subscription**: the live connection between a source and the code that listens to it; to dispose it is to close it; owner [22](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md) ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **subscribable**: any object with a `subscribe` method, such as an RxJS `Observable`; a cold one starts its work for each subscriber ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **`markForCheck`**: tells Angular a view must be re-checked; owner [21](../modules/21-change-detection.md) ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **`ErrorHandler`**: the application's central error sink; owner [37](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md) ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **zone.js / zone-based / zoneless (as used in 14)**: zone.js (ZoneJS in the CHANGELOG) patches the browser's async APIs so Angular learns something may have changed; a zone-based app loads it, a zoneless one does not; owner [17 §1](../modules/17-signals.md#1-why-signals-exist) ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **`TestBed` (as used in 14)**: Angular's test harness, which creates a component in a test and rethrows application errors by default; owner [13 §9](../modules/13-components-and-templates.md#9-view-encapsulation-emulated-shadowdom-and-none-and-what-the-compiler-does-to-your-css) ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **strategy (pattern)**: one interface with interchangeable algorithms; `AsyncPipe` picks a promise or subscribable strategy ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **`async` pipe**: an impure pipe that subscribes, stores the latest value, marks the view, and disposes on source change or destroy ([14 §8](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#8-the-async-pipe-under-the-hood)).
- **bubbling**: the order in which a DOM event reaches the clicked element first and then each ancestor ([14 Exercise 14.1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-1)).
- **`stopImmediatePropagation`**: the DOM event method that stops the remaining listeners on the same element and the event's trip to ancestors ([14 Exercise 14.1](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-1)).
- **`CurrentRoles`**: the exercise's small injectable that holds a signal of the user's roles, a stand-in for a permission backend ([14 Exercise 14.2](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-2)).
- **`recorder` (as used in 14.3)**: the exercise's shared array into which each constructor pushes its name, so a test can assert creation order ([14 Exercise 14.3](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-3)).
- **normalizer (as used in 14.4)**: a function that maps text to a comparable form (here lower-casing); the exercise injects it through `SEARCH_NORMALIZER` ([14 Exercise 14.4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-4)).
- **counting normalizer**: a normalizer that adds one to a counter per call, so a test counts work without timers ([14 Exercise 14.4](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-4)).
- **structural type (as used in 14.5)**: a type matched by the members an object has, here `Subscribable<T>` (an object with `subscribe({ next, error })`), so the model needs no RxJS ([14 Exercise 14.5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-5); concept owner [06 §2](../modules/06-ts-type-system-essentials.md#2-structural-typing-assignability-and-readonly)).
- **counting source**: a test source that records how many times it was subscribed to and unsubscribed from, so a test counts work without timers ([14 Exercise 14.5](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#ex14-5)).
- **slot (pipe)**: the cell a view keeps for one pipe expression, holding its last arguments and result ([14 Q14.17](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#q14-17)).
- **digit pattern**: the `min-int.min-frac-max-frac` string (`3.1-1`) that `number`, `percent` and `currency` take to set integer and fraction digits ([14 Q14.22](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#q14-22)).
- **late value (as used in Q14.27)**: a value a source emits after the `async` pipe has replaced it with another source; the pipe ignores it ([14 Q14.27](../modules/14-directives-and-pipes.md#q14-27)).

## Module 16

- **dependency injection (DI)**: a class declares what it needs and an injector decides what it gets ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **dependency**: anything a class requires but does not create ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **inversion of control**: the reversal in which the container, not the class, controls how a dependency is created ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **injector**: the object that resolves a token through a provider and caches the result ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **token**: the key a consumer asks an injector for: a class, an abstract class or an `InjectionToken`; typing one is [07 Q07.18](../modules/07-ts-advanced-types-and-decorators.md#q07-18) ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **provider**: the recipe that tells an injector how to produce the value for a token ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **singleton (as used in 16)**: one instance shared by every consumer of an injector, because the injector caches it ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **injection context**: a place where `inject()` may be called, such as construction or a field initializer; owner [16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync) ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **`@Service`**: the v22 decorator the docs call a shorthand for `@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })`; it rejects constructor parameters, owner [16 §3](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking) ([16 §1](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#1-why-dependency-injection-containers-constructor-injection-and-inject)).
- **`useClass`**: the provider recipe that constructs a class for a token; two tokens on one class give two instances ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **`useValue`**: the provider recipe that hands over one given value, stored by reference ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **`useFactory`**: the provider recipe that calls a factory function in an injection context, once per injector ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **`useExisting`**: the provider recipe that makes an alias, answering with the instance of another token ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **factory function**: a function that returns the value for a token; Angular calls it on first lookup ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **alias (as used in 16)**: a second token that answers with the instance of another token, made by `useExisting` ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **last wins**: with several providers for one token in one injector, the last one answers and the others are dropped without an error ([16 §2](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#2-providers-and-tokens-useclass-usevalue-usefactory-and-useexisting)).
- **`providedIn`**: the `@Injectable` option that registers the class's recipe on the class itself, in the root or platform injector, instead of in a `providers` array ([16 §3](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- **platform injector**: the singleton injector above the root that the typings say is "shared by all applications on the page"; `providedIn: 'platform'` registers there ([16 §3](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- **`autoProvided`**: the `@Service` option; `false` leaves the class unregistered until a `providers` array lists it ([16 §3](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- **tree-shaking**: the build dropping code that nothing reaches; for a root service, the docs promise it when the code never uses it, and one scratch build kept unused classes reached through an index ([16 §3](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- **field initializer**: the expression after `=` in a class body, run while the object is constructed, so `inject()` works there ([16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- **provider factory**: the function behind `useFactory`, a `@Service` `factory` or an `InjectionToken` `factory`; it runs in an injection context ([16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- **lifecycle hook**: a method such as `ngOnInit` that Angular calls at set moments; it is not an injection context, owner module 20 ([16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- **`assertInInjectionContext`**: the helper a function that wraps `inject()` calls first, so a call outside a context throws `NG0203` naming that function ([16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- **`runInInjectionContext`**: the free function `runInInjectionContext(injector, fn)` that makes `injector` current while `fn` runs synchronously; there is no `Injector` method of that name in the 22.2.1 typings ([16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- **`injectAsync`**: the v22.0 helper that returns a function giving a promise of a lazily loaded, auto-provided service; the loader runs once and each call returns a new promise ([16 §4](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#4-the-injection-context-inject-runininjectioncontext-and-injectasync)).
- **element injector**: the injector that belongs to one element, empty unless its component or directive lists `providers`; searched first ([16 §5](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- **environment injector**: an injector for a region of the app (platform, root, or one per route with `providers`), searched after the element tree ([16 §5](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- **resolution**: the walk a lookup makes: up the element injectors, then the environment injectors, until a provider answers or the `NullInjector` ends it ([16 §5](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- **modifier**: an `inject()` option (`optional`, `self`, `skipSelf`, `host`) that changes where the walk starts or ends, or whether a miss returns `null` ([16 §5](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- **`NullInjector`**: the top of the injector tree; a lookup that reaches it throws `NG0201`, or returns the fallback or `null` when one is given ([16 §5](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- **route injector**: the environment injector a route creates when it lists `providers`; siblings get separate ones and a revisit reuses it, routing owned by module 24 ([16 §5](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#5-the-injector-tree-platform-root-route-and-element-injectors-and-the-modifiers)).
- **`providers`**: the component metadata array of providers visible to its view and its projected content ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **`viewProviders`**: the component metadata array of providers visible only to the component's own view, not to projected content ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **view**: the template a component declares; its counterpart is content ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **content (projected)**: the markup a parent writes between a component's tags and the component shows through `<ng-content>`; owner module 19 ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **per-instance state**: data owned by one component instance, given by listing its class in the component's `providers` ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **`HostAttributeToken`**: a token that injects a static attribute of the host element, `null` when optional and absent ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **`HOST_TAG_NAME`**: a token that injects the host element's tag name ([16 §6](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#6-providers-versus-viewproviders-and-per-instance-state)).
- **`InjectionToken`**: a typed key created with a description, for a value that has no class; a `factory` makes it provide itself in root ([16 §7](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- **multi provider**: a provider with `multi: true`; every such provider for one token appends to one array, kept per injector ([16 §7](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- **`EnvironmentProviders`**: the branded wrapper type for providers only an environment injector may hold; components refuse it ([16 §7](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- **`makeEnvironmentProviders`**: the function that wraps a list of providers into `EnvironmentProviders` ([16 §7](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- **initializer**: a function an injector runs when it is created; environment initializers are not awaited, app initializers are, owner [12 Q12.17](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-17) ([16 §7](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- **`provideEnvironmentInitializer`**: the v19.0 function that runs a function once, in an injection context, when an environment injector is created; it replaces the deprecated `ENVIRONMENT_INITIALIZER` token ([16 §7](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#7-injectiontoken-multi-providers-environment-providers-and-initializers)).
- **`ngOnDestroy`**: the method Angular calls on an instance when its owner (an injector or a component) is destroyed; hooks in general are owned by module 20 ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **`DestroyRef`**: the v16.0 handle whose `onDestroy(callback)` registers cleanup for the scope it was injected in (a component, or an injector) and returns an unregister function ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **lazy instantiation**: an injector builds an instance on the first request for its token, not when the injector is created ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **`createEnvironmentInjector`**: the function that builds an environment injector at run time from providers and a required parent; `destroy()` ends it ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **`Injector.create`**: the static method that builds a lighter injector from `Provider | StaticProvider` and an optional parent; its result has `destroy()` since v20.0 ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **forward reference**: `forwardRef(() => X)`, a wrapper that delays reading a class declared later; it does not break a circular dependency ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **circular dependency**: two tokens that need each other, so neither can be built; it fails at run time as `NG0200` with a path ([16 §8](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#8-lifetime-and-dynamic-injectors-destroyref-environmentinjector-injectorcreate-forward-references-and-cycles)).
- **strategy**: one interface with interchangeable implementations behind a token; the injector picks one ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **facade**: one class in front of several services, so consumers learn a single method ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **adapter**: a class that gives a foreign API the shape a token promises, so consumers keep one interface ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **barrel**: an index file that re-exports other files; one scratch build kept unused services re-exported through one ([16 §3](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#3-providedin-service-scopes-and-tree-shaking)).
- **`TestBed` (as used in 16)**: Angular's testing harness; its providers and `overrideProvider` replace a root service, and only `overrideComponent` reaches a component's own `providers`; owner module 29 ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **configuration object**: a token holding typed settings, with a factory default when one is safe ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **feature flag**: a token whose value, or whose factory's choice, selects an implementation ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **`provide*` function**: a function such as `provideBilling()` that returns `EnvironmentProviders` to configure a feature in one call ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).
- **scoped proxy**: Spring's stand-in injected into a longer-lived bean that fetches the real shorter-lived bean from its scope; Spring term, documented only ([16 §9](../modules/16-dependency-injection.md#9-di-patterns-testing-and-the-spring-container)).

## Module 15

- **NgModule**: a class decorated with `@NgModule` that groups declarables and providers; it gave declared components a compilation scope, gave an injector providers, and was the unit the router lazy loaded ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **declarable**: a component, directive or pipe, the kinds of class an NgModule can declare ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **compilation scope (template scope)**: the selectors and pipe names a component's template may use ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **`declarations`**: the NgModule metadata listing the declarables that belong to the module ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **`exports` (NgModule)**: the declarables (or modules) an NgModule makes usable in the templates of modules that import it ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **root module**: the NgModule an app bootstraps; its providers go to the root injector ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **feature module**: an NgModule that groups one feature ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **lazy-loaded module**: an NgModule the router loads on demand with `loadChildren`, with its own injector ([15 §1](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#1-what-ngmodules-did-compilation-scope-injector-and-lazy-loading-unit)).
- **standalone component**: a component that lists its own template dependencies in `imports` and needs no NgModule; the default since v19.0 ([15 §2](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- **`imports` (component)**: the metadata listing a standalone component's template dependencies: standalone components, directives, pipes and NgModules (whose exports enter the scope) ([15 §2](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- **`standalone: false`**: the opt-out that lets a component, directive or pipe be declared in an NgModule ([15 §2](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- **`isStandalone`**: a function that returns the `standalone` field of a component, directive or pipe definition, `false` for anything else ([15 §2](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- **`strictStandalone`**: an `angularCompilerOptions` flag that rejects every `standalone: false` (`NG2023`) ([15 §2](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- **`schemas` (component)**: standalone-only metadata declaring which unknown elements and properties a template may contain ([15 §2](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#2-standalone-components-and-the-imports-array)).
- **`bootstrapApplication`**: the function that starts a standalone app: it builds the root environment injector from an `ApplicationConfig` and renders the root component ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **`ApplicationConfig`**: an object with one `providers` array for the root environment injector, by convention in `app.config.ts`; exported from `@angular/core` ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **`bootstrapModule`**: the `PlatformRef` method that starts an NgModule app from its root module, rendering the components in `bootstrap` ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **application providers (`applicationProviders`)**: the `bootstrapModule` option that adds root providers to an NgModule app without editing the module ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **`provide*` function**: a function such as `provideRouter` that returns the `EnvironmentProviders` one feature needs, replacing a provider-carrying NgModule ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **`importProvidersFrom`**: a function that collects the providers of NgModules (and their imported modules and `ModuleWithProviders`) into `EnvironmentProviders` for an environment injector ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **platform**: the page-wide Angular layer that `platformBrowser()` returns and on which applications are bootstrapped ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom); owned by [12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).
- **`NgModuleRef`**: the handle to a running NgModule, returned by `bootstrapModule`; its `instance` is the module and its `injector` the module's injector ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
