- **lazy loading**: downloading a feature's code only when a navigation first needs it ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- **`loadComponent`**: a route property whose function loads one standalone component, usually with a dynamic `import()` ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- **`loadChildren`**: a route property whose function loads a `Routes` array (or, in legacy code, an NgModule) for the route's children ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- **`Routes`**: the router's array of route entries (paths, components, loaders, providers) ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays); owned by [module 24](../modules/24-routing.md)).
- **route `providers`**: providers listed on a route, from which the router creates that route's environment injector once ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- **chunk**: a separately downloaded JavaScript file the bundler emits, for example for each dynamic `import()` target ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- **default export (lazy loading)**: an `export default` that the router unwraps, so a loader can return `import('./file')` without `.then` ([15 §4](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#4-lazy-loading-without-modules-loadcomponent-and-loadchildren-with-route-arrays)).
- **`ModuleWithProviders`**: an object `{ ngModule, providers? }` that pairs an NgModule with extra providers; what `forRoot()` and `forChild()` return ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **`forRoot`**: by convention, a static NgModule method that returns the module plus its root-only providers (configuration, singletons) ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **`forChild`**: by convention, a static NgModule method that returns the module without the root-only providers, for feature and lazy modules ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **re-export**: listing in an NgModule's `exports` a module it imports, so importers also get that module's exports ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **`SharedModule`**: the habit of one NgModule that declares and re-exports the declarables every feature needs; giving it providers was the anti-pattern ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **`CoreModule`**: an NgModule holding app-wide singletons, imported only by the root module and guarded against a second import ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **module constructor**: the constructor of an NgModule class, run when the injector that imports the module is created ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **anti-pattern**: a habit that looks right and causes a defect ([15 §5](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#5-legacy-ngmodule-patterns-declarations-exports-forrootforchild-sharedmodule-and-coremodule)).
- **interop (standalone and NgModules)**: the rules that let standalone components and NgModules use each other: a standalone component imports an NgModule, an NgModule imports a standalone class ([15 §6](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- **hybrid app**: an app that mixes NgModules and standalone components, as every migration does for a while ([15 §6](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- **standalone injector**: the environment injector Angular creates, once per component definition, for a standalone component created as a root view whose imports carry providers; a child of the current environment injector ([15 §6](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- **library (Angular)**: a package of Angular code for other apps, published partially compiled and finished by the CLI's linker ([15 §6](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)); the linker is owned by [12](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-28).
- **standalone migration (schematic)**: `ng generate @angular/core:standalone`, the schematic that converts an NgModule app in three passes (`convert-to-standalone`, `prune-ng-modules`, `standalone-bootstrap`) ([15 §7](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures)).
- **migration pass**: one run of the standalone schematic in one mode, after which the app should build and its tests pass ([15 §7](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures)).
- **follow-up schematic**: a migration run after the three passes to finish the job, such as `route-lazy-loading`, `cleanup-unused-imports` or `common-to-standalone` ([15 §7](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures)).
- **`ɵmod`**: the module definition the compiler writes onto an NgModule class; after AOT it holds only the type (internal API) ([15 §8](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- **`ɵinj`**: the injector definition the compiler writes onto an NgModule class: its `providers` and `imports` (internal API) ([15 §8](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- **`ngJitMode`**: a global that an optimized build sets to `false`; code guarded by it, such as `ɵɵsetNgModuleScope`, runs only when just-in-time compilation is possible ([15 §8](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector); [Q12.15](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-15)).
- **`StandaloneService`**: the internal service, one per environment injector, that creates and caches standalone injectors and destroys them with its injector ([15 §8](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- **`TestBed`**: Angular's test harness, which builds a testing module and creates components in a test; owner module 29 ([15 §6](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#6-interop-ngmodule-libraries-in-standalone-apps-and-standalone-pieces-in-ngmodule-apps)).
- **AOT (ahead-of-time compilation)**: compiling templates and decorators at build time, so a module's scope is written into each declared component; the `ng test` build does it too ([15 §7](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#7-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorestandalone-three-passes-hybrid-apps-and-common-failures); owner [Q12.19](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-19)).
- **JIT (just-in-time compilation)**: compiling templates and decorators in the browser at run time, which is why `ɵɵsetNgModuleScope` still registers a module's scope when `ngJitMode` allows it ([15 §8](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#8-under-the-hood-what-the-compiler-emits-for-modules-and-standalone-components-and-the-standalone-injector)).
- **zoneless (as used in 15)**: change detection without zone.js; `bootstrapModule` builds its root injector with the zoneless providers first; owner [module 21](../modules/21-change-detection.md) ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom)).
- **`ApplicationRef` (as used in 15)**: the app's root handle, which lives in the root environment injector ([15 §3](../modules/15-standalone-and-ngmodules.md#3-bootstrapping-and-providers-without-modules-bootstrapapplication-provide-functions-and-importprovidersfrom); owner [12 §6](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#6-bootstrapping-bootstrapapplication-the-platform-and-initializers)).

## Module 18

- **control flow block**: template syntax (`@if`, `@for`, `@switch`, `@defer`) that the compiler understands, in place of a directive ([18 §1](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as))
- **embedded view**: a piece of template with its own DOM nodes and bindings that Angular creates and destroys as a unit; each block branch is one ([18 §1](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as))
- **alias (`as`)**: a name for the value of an `@if` or `@else if` condition, visible only in the branch it came from ([18 §1](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as))
- **`ngc`**: the Angular compiler's command line, used here to observe diagnostics and emitted code ([18 §1](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#1-built-in-blocks-versus-directives-and-the-if-family-with-as))
- **`track` (key)**: the expression in `@for` that computes each item's key; Angular matches old and new rows by it ([18 §2](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- **row**: one embedded view that a `@for` creates for one item ([18 §2](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- **context variable**: a name such as `$index` or `$first` that a `@for` row provides, aliased with `let` ([18 §2](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- **`@empty`**: the block of a `@for` that renders when the collection has no items ([18 §2](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- **iterable**: a JavaScript object with a `[Symbol.iterator]()` method (array, `Set`, `Map`, string, generator), which `@for` accepts ([18 §2](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#2-for-track-context-variables-empty-and-what-angular-can-iterate))
- **exhaustiveness (of a `@switch`)**: every member of the switched union has a `@case`, proved at compile time by `@default never;` ([18 §3](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#3-switch-and-exhaustive-checking-with-default-never))
- **`@default never`**: a body-less last block that makes the type checker assign the switched value to `never`, so a missing case fails the build ([18 §3](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#3-switch-and-exhaustive-checking-with-default-never))
- **type-check block (TCB)**: the TypeScript the Angular compiler generates from a template so that TypeScript can check it ([18 §3](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#3-switch-and-exhaustive-checking-with-default-never); owner [12 §7](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#7-aot-versus-jit-and-template-type-checking))
- **declaration view (of a block)**: the view whose template declared a block; the block reads `@let` values from it ([18 §4](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#4-let-inside-blocks-scope-reactivity-and-when-it-helps))
- **deferrable view**: a template part wrapped in `@defer`, whose code loads later and which moves between placeholder, loading, complete and error ([18 §5](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- **placeholder, loading and error blocks**: the optional `@placeholder`, `@loading` and `@error` sub-blocks of a `@defer`, one per state besides the content ([18 §5](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- **`minimum` and `after`**: `after` delays entering the loading state; `minimum` holds a state once entered ([18 §5](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- **`PendingTasks`**: Angular's counter of unfinished work that tests and the server wait for ([18 §5](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- **`ErrorHandler`**: Angular's error-reporting service; its default logs to the console ([18 §5](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#5-defer-blocks-and-states-placeholder-loading-error-minimum-and-after))
- **trigger**: the event or condition that starts a `@defer` load or render (`on idle`, `on viewport`, `on interaction`, `on hover`, `on immediate`, `on timer`, `when`) ([18 §6](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- **prefetch**: a second trigger, `prefetch on …` or `prefetch when …`, that fetches a `@defer` block's code without rendering it ([18 §6](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- **`requestIdleCallback`**: the browser call that runs work when the page is idle; the `idle` trigger uses it, with a `setTimeout` fallback ([18 §6](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- **`IdleService`**: the interface behind the `idle` trigger, replaceable with `provideIdleServiceWith` ([18 §6](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#6-triggers-and-prefetching))
- **`DepsFn`**: the arrow function the compiler passes to `ɵɵdefer`; it returns a dynamic `import()` for a deferrable dependency and the class itself for an eager one ([18 §7](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- **barrel**: an `index.ts` that re-exports other files, so one import can reach several of them ([18 §7](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- **`DeferBlockBehavior`**: the `TestBed` option that makes `@defer` blocks play through like a browser (`Playthrough`, the runtime default) or wait for the test (`Manual`) ([18 §7](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- **`DeferBlockFixture`**: the test handle for one `@defer` block, from `getDeferBlocks()`; `render(state)` moves it to a state ([18 §7](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- **hydration (as used in 18)**: the browser reusing the DOM the server rendered instead of building it again; owned by module 32 ([18 §7](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- **`ngServerMode`**: the global flag Angular checks to know it is rendering on a server ([18 §7](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#7-what-lands-in-a-chunk-testing-deferred-blocks-and-the-ssr-pointer))
- **schematic**: a code generator or code rewriter that the Angular CLI runs, such as `ng generate @angular/core:control-flow` ([18 §8](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- **codemod**: a program that rewrites source code mechanically ([18 §8](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- **hybrid template**: a template that mixes directives and blocks, as after a migration that keeps a referenced `ng-template` ([18 §8](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- **`--path` (control flow schematic)**: the directory under the project root the schematic migrates, default `./` ([18 §8](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- **`CommonModule` (as used in 18)**: the NgModule that bundles `NgIf`, `NgForOf`, `NgSwitch` and the common pipes, no longer needed for the blocks ([18 §8](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#8-migrating-with-ng-generate-angularcorecontrol-flow))
- **`LView` and `LContainer`**: the runtime arrays that hold one view's nodes and binding values, and the embedded views of one block ([18 §9](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- **`ɵɵ` instruction**: a function the compiler emits calls to, such as `ɵɵconditional`; internal, never called from application code ([18 §9](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- **`reconcile`**: the runtime function that compares a `@for` collection with the live views by key and keeps, moves, creates or destroys views ([18 §9](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- **detached view**: a view taken out of its container without being destroyed, kept by key so a later row can re-attach it ([18 §9](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))
- **idle bucket**: the group of `@defer` idle callbacks that share one timeout and one idle request in the `IdleScheduler` ([18 §9](../modules/18-control-flow-and-defer.md#9-under-the-hood-what-the-blocks-compile-to-how-for-reconciles-and-how-defer-moves-between-states))

## Module 19

- **contract (of a component)**: the inputs and outputs a component declares, which the compiler checks every parent against ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **input**: a property a parent binds with `[x]`; data flows down through it ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **output**: an event a child announces and the parent handles with `(x)`; not a DOM event and it does not bubble ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **model**: an input plus an output named `xChange` behind one `[(x)]` ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **`transform` (input option)**: a function that converts the bound value before it is stored ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **`alias` (input option)**: a public binding name different from the field name ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **`strictTemplates`**: the compiler option that type-checks template bindings against the declared types ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **`ngc`**: the Angular compiler's command line, used here to observe diagnostics and emitted code ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **OnPush**: a component Angular refreshes only when something marks it dirty; the default for a component with no `changeDetection` property ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **`EventEmitter`**: the RxJS `Subject` subclass that decorator outputs use ([19 §1](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#1-inputs-outputs-and-two-way-binding-the-parent-and-child-contract))
- **service**: a class Angular injects into components and other classes ([19 §2](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- **event bus**: a `Subject` that any component can emit on and subscribe to; it has no current value ([19 §2](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- **lifting state up**: moving a value to the nearest common parent of the components that need it ([19 §2](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- **provider scope**: the injector where a class is registered; a class is one instance per injector, not per application ([19 §2](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#2-sharing-state-beyond-the-tree-services-with-signals-and-di-scoped-state))
- **content projection**: the markup a caller writes between a component's tags, shown where the component places `<ng-content>` ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **slot (content projection)**: one `<ng-content>` placeholder of a component ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **`select`**: the selector (element, attribute or class) a node must match to enter an `<ng-content>` slot ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **wildcard slot**: an `<ng-content>` without `select`, which takes the nodes no other slot claimed ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **fallback content**: the content inside an `<ng-content>` tag, shown when no node matched the slot ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **declaring template**: the template in which a piece of content is written, which creates its nodes and owns them for change detection ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **`ngProjectAs`**: an attribute that makes a node match slots by a given selector instead of its own identity; static values only ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **extended diagnostic**: a compiler check beyond the type check, such as `NG8011`, reported as a warning by default ([12](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-21)) ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **template fragment**: markup inside `<ng-template>` that Angular creates only when something renders it ([19 §4](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- **context (of a template)**: the object a renderer supplies to a template; `$implicit` feeds a bare `let-x` and other properties feed `let-x="name"` ([19 §4](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- **render prop**: a function a component calls to draw part of itself, the React idiom nearest to passing a template ([19 §4](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- **marker directive**: a directive that exists to be found by a query and to carry options, such as a column's header ([19 §4](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#4-template-driven-apis-ng-template-ngtemplateoutlet-and-typed-context))
- **query**: a lookup Angular keeps up to date for the elements, components or directives a component declares or projects ([19 §5](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- **locator**: what a query searches for: a class, a template reference name or any provider token ([19 §5](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- **view query**: a query over the elements of the component's own template; never into another component's template ([19 §5](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- **content query**: a query over the content the caller projected between the component's tags ([19 §5](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- **`read` (query option)**: the option that picks which value to take from the matched element, such as `ElementRef`, `TemplateRef` or `Injector` ([19 §5](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- **`descendants` (query option)**: whether a content query looks beyond the host's direct children; `contentChildren` defaults to no, `contentChild` to yes ([19 §5](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#5-queries-view-and-content-queries-with-signal-queries))
- **decorator (Angular)**: a function applied to a class member with `@` that registers it as an input, output or query ([19 §6](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- **`static` query**: a decorator query option that resolves the result early, for an element outside any embedded view ([19 §6](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- **`QueryList`**: the live collection a plural decorator query gives, with `changes`, `first`, `last`, `length`, `get` and `toArray` ([19 §6](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- **codemod**: a program that rewrites source code; a schematic is the CLI's form ([19 §6](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- **`--best-effort-mode`**: a schematic option that migrates even what it cannot prove safe, so the build may break ([19 §6](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#6-decorator-based-inputs-outputs-and-queries-and-the-migration-schematics))
- **dynamic component**: a component that code creates at run time instead of a template ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- **view container**: a place in a template that holds views; `ViewContainerRef` is its handle ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- **host view**: the view that wraps a created component, which `ComponentRef.hostView` returns ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- **`ApplicationRef`**: the object that runs change detection for the application's root views and holds the views attached to it ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- **binding function**: `inputBinding`, `outputBinding` or `twoWayBinding`, passed in `bindings` when a component is created in code ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- **`projectableNodes`**: the creation option that gives one array of DOM nodes per slot of a dynamic component ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))
- **`ɵcmp`**: the static definition the compiler writes on a component class; internal, never to be used directly ([19 §8](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh); [12](../modules/12-angular-how-it-works.md#q12-24))
- **instruction**: a function the runtime calls from a compiled template to create or update a view ([19 §8](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- **`TNode`**: the static, per-template description of a template node, as opposed to the node of one rendered view ([19 §8](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- **dirty counter**: the signal a query signal reads and the runtime bumps when the query's results may have changed ([19 §8](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- **`ComponentMirror`**: the public description of a component that `reflectComponentType` returns: selector, inputs, outputs and slot selectors ([19 §8](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#8-under-the-hood-the-compiled-definition-projection-slots-and-query-refresh))
- **Shadow DOM**: the browser's encapsulated DOM subtree, which the native `<slot>` belongs to ([19 §3](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#3-content-projection-ng-content-slots-ngprojectas-fallback-content-and-the-lifecycle-trap))
- **`tick`**: the `ApplicationRef` method that runs one change-detection pass ([19 §7](../modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md#7-dynamic-components-viewcontainerrefcreatecomponent-createcomponent-and-bindings))

## Module 20

- **lifecycle hook**: a method Angular calls on a component at a fixed point of change detection, such as `ngOnInit` or `ngOnDestroy` ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **change detection**: the walk over the component tree that checks the template bindings of each view it refreshes ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **pre-order**: a node visited before its children; `ngOnChanges`, `ngOnInit` and `ngDoCheck` run this way ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **post-order**: a node visited after its children; the view hooks run this way, so a child's `ngAfterViewInit` precedes its parent's ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **declaring view**: the template that contains a component's tag; a component's hooks run when that view is checked, not its own ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **zoneless**: change detection driven by signals and explicit notifications instead of zone.js patching browser events; how the lab's TestBed runs ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **constructor (of a component)**: plain class construction; the injection context is open and inputs still hold their defaults ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **injection context**: the moment `inject()` is allowed: construction, a field initializer or `runInInjectionContext` ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **field initializer**: a class field's `= expression`, which runs during construction ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **required input**: an input declared with `input.required()`; it has no default and throws `NG0950` if read before it is bound ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **`SimpleChange`**: the record of one input change: `previousValue`, `currentValue` and `firstChange` ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **`SimpleChanges`**: the object `ngOnChanges` receives, keyed by input property name, one `SimpleChange` per changed input ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **alias (of an input)**: a public binding name different from the property name; `SimpleChanges` still uses the property name ([20 §2](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#2-constructor-ngoninit-and-inputs-what-exists-when))
- **derived state**: a value computed from other state, best held in a `computed` rather than copied in a hook ([20 §3](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- **custom change detection**: a component's own comparison in `ngDoCheck`, run when its declaring view is checked ([20 §3](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- **in-place mutation**: editing an object's properties without replacing it, which no signal or input reference announces ([20 §3](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- **untracked read**: a signal read that registers no dependency; lifecycle hooks run this way ([20 §3](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#3-which-hooks-still-matter-with-signals))
- **render callback**: a function registered with `afterNextRender` or `afterEveryRender` that Angular calls after it has finished rendering the whole application ([20 §4](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))
- **phase (of a render hook)**: one of `earlyRead`, `write`, `mixedReadWrite` and `read`; each runs across all components before the next starts ([20 §4](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))
- **`manualCleanup`**: the render-hook option that keeps the hook alive after its owner is destroyed until `AfterRenderRef.destroy()` is called ([20 §4](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))
- **teardown**: what runs when a component is destroyed: its `ngOnDestroy`, its `DestroyRef` callbacks and its effect cleanups ([20 §5](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed))
- **unregister function**: the function `DestroyRef.onDestroy` returns; calling it removes the callback ([20 §5](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed))
- **late work**: asynchronous work that finishes after its owner was destroyed ([20 §5](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed))
- **development mode**: a build where `ngDevMode` is true, which adds checks such as the second pass behind `NG0100`; production builds drop them ([20 §6](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#6-expressionchangedafterithasbeencheckederror-cause-and-fixes))
- **binding (template)**: a template expression tied to a value shown on screen ([20 §6](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#6-expressionchangedafterithasbeencheckederror-cause-and-fixes))
- **stale (view)**: a view that shows an older value than the state it reads ([20 §6](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#6-expressionchangedafterithasbeencheckederror-cause-and-fixes))
- **view**: the runtime record of one template instance, split into a static `TView` and a live `LView` (both internal) ([20 §7](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#7-under-the-hood-hook-lists-the-view-refresh-and-the-render-hook-queue))
- **init phase**: the 0 to 3 counter in a view's flags that makes `Init` hooks run once and `Checked` hooks on every refresh ([20 §7](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#7-under-the-hood-hook-lists-the-view-refresh-and-the-render-hook-queue))
- **dirty flag (of the application)**: a bit on `ApplicationRef` meaning a pass is owed; any dirty pass also sets the after-render flag, so the render queue runs even when no view was refreshed ([20 §7](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#7-under-the-hood-hook-lists-the-view-refresh-and-the-render-hook-queue))
- **OnPush (as used in 20)**: a component Angular refreshes only when something marks it dirty; the default since v22.0, owner [21](../modules/21-change-detection.md) ([20 §1](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#1-the-lifecycle-map-phases-and-the-order-of-the-hooks))
- **`ngServerMode` (as used in 20)**: the internal global flag server rendering sets; render hooks return a no-op ref under it ([20 §4](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#4-render-hooks-afternextrender-aftereveryrender-and-their-phases))

## Module 21

- **change detection**: the step that makes the DOM match the state: each binding of a refreshed view is evaluated and compared with its last value, and the DOM is written only where it differs ([21 §1](../modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity))
- **dirty checking**: comparing each binding with its last value after anything might have changed ([21 §1](../modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity))
- **trigger (of change detection)**: what tells Angular to look at the views: a zone notification, a signal write, `markForCheck` or an event ([21 §1](../modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity))
- **selection (of change detection)**: which views a notification refreshes, decided by the strategy and the dirty marks ([21 §1](../modules/21-change-detection.md#1-the-synchronization-problem-dirty-checking-and-fine-grained-reactivity))
- **strategy (change detection)**: a component setting that says when its view is checked: `OnPush` only when marked, `Eager` whenever the walk reaches it ([21 §2](../modules/21-change-detection.md#2-onpush-and-eager-strategies-and-what-marks-a-view-dirty))
- **dirty (view)**: flagged for a check by a mark such as a new input value, an event, a signal change or `markForCheck` ([21 §2](../modules/21-change-detection.md#2-onpush-and-eager-strategies-and-what-marks-a-view-dirty))
- **`setInput`**: `ComponentRef.setInput`, which sets a component input from code and marks the component's view ([21 §2](../modules/21-change-detection.md#2-onpush-and-eager-strategies-and-what-marks-a-view-dirty))
- **monkey patch**: replacing a function of another object at run time, as zone.js does with the browser's async APIs ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **zone / zone.js**: an execution context that sees its tasks start and end, and the library that patches the browser's async APIs so callbacks run in one ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **`NgZone`**: the zone Angular forks from zone.js; it emits `onUnstable`, `onMicrotaskEmpty` and `onStable` and, in a zone-based app, triggers a tick ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **`onMicrotaskEmpty` / `onStable`**: `NgZone` events emitted when no entry is open and no microtask is pending, the first inside the zone and the second outside it ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **tick**: one change detection run over the views attached to the `ApplicationRef` ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **event coalescing**: `provideZoneChangeDetection({ eventCoalescing: true })`, which merges the ticks of the listeners of one event into one ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **polyfills (build)**: the entries a build loads before the application, where zone.js was typically listed ([21 §3](../modules/21-change-detection.md#3-zonejs-and-ngzone-how-changes-used-to-be-detected-and-why-zone-left-the-default))
- **refresh (of a view)**: a view's template running during the walk ([21 §4](../modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- **root view**: the view that hosts a root component, where each walk starts ([21 §4](../modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- **global mode / targeted mode**: the two modes of the walk: global refreshes every `Eager` (`CheckAlways`) view and every flagged view, targeted only views flagged `RefreshView` or whose signal reader is dirty ([21 §4](../modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- **`RefreshView` / `CheckAlways` / `HasChildViewsToRefresh`**: internal view flags: refresh this view now, refresh whenever the walk is global, and pass through this view to reach a flagged descendant ([21 §4](../modules/21-change-detection.md#4-the-tree-walk-global-and-targeted-refreshes))
- **notification (change detection)**: a call to the scheduler saying some state may need a render, made by a signal read in a template, `setInput`, a listener, `markForCheck`, an attached view or a render hook ([21 §5](../modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- **scheduler (change detection)**: the service that sets dirty flags on notifications and schedules one tick, racing `setTimeout` against `requestAnimationFrame` ([21 §5](../modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- **stability / pending task**: the app is stable when no pending task is open; a pending task is a unit in the app's counter of unfinished work, added by `PendingTasks` and by the scheduler itself ([21 §5](../modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- **`PendingTasks`**: the service whose `add()` and `run()` keep the app unstable until a task is done ([21 §5](../modules/21-change-detection.md#5-zoneless-change-detection-the-scheduler-its-triggers-and-pendingtasks))
- **silent update**: a state change that no notification announces, so no view is scheduled to refresh ([21 §6](../modules/21-change-detection.md#6-migrating-a-zone-based-app-to-zoneless))
- **OnPush-compatible**: code that announces every change its template shows, through a signal, the `async` pipe, `markForCheck`, an input or an event ([21 §6](../modules/21-change-detection.md#6-migrating-a-zone-based-app-to-zoneless))
- **hybrid app (zone)**: an application that still provides `provideZoneChangeDetection` beside the notification scheduler ([21 §6](../modules/21-change-detection.md#6-migrating-a-zone-based-app-to-zoneless))
- **view reference**: a handle on one view; `ChangeDetectorRef` is its public type and `ViewRef` the class behind it, with the verbs `markForCheck`, `detectChanges`, `detach` and `reattach` ([21 §7](../modules/21-change-detection.md#7-changedetectorref-markforcheck-detectchanges-detach-and-reattach))
- **attached / detached (view)**: a view is attached while the tree walk may visit it and detached after `detach()`, until `reattach()` ([21 §7](../modules/21-change-detection.md#7-changedetectorref-markforcheck-detectchanges-detach-and-reattach))
- **ancestor path**: the parents of a view up to the root; `markForCheck` flags it and a signal does not ([21 §7](../modules/21-change-detection.md#7-changedetectorref-markforcheck-detectchanges-detach-and-reattach))
- **profiler**: a tool that records when the framework's change detection events start and end, such as the Angular DevTools Profiler tab or the Chrome performance track ([21 §8](../modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- **flame graph**: the DevTools Profiler view where each tile is an element, the x-axis is time and the y-axis the element hierarchy ([21 §8](../modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- **exhaustive check**: `provideCheckNoChangesConfig({ exhaustive: true })`, which re-checks every view as if it were `Eager`, so it also reports plain-field writes hidden by `OnPush`; its `interval` repeats the check on a timer ([21 §8](../modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- **task tracking (zone.js)**: a zone.js plugin that records scheduled macrotasks, which `provideStabilityDebugging` logs when the app does not stabilize ([21 §8](../modules/21-change-detection.md#8-debugging-the-profiler-providechecknochangesconfig-and-stability))
- **view flag / dirty flag**: a view flag is an internal bit on a view (`Dirty`, `RefreshView`, `HasChildViewsToRefresh`) saying it needs a refresh; a dirty flag is an internal bit on the `ApplicationRef` (`ViewTreeGlobal`, `ViewTreeTraversal`, `ViewTreeCheck`, `AfterRender`, `RootEffects`) saying a pass is owed ([21 §9](../modules/21-change-detection.md#9-under-the-hood-the-notification-the-dirty-flags-and-the-refresh))
- **live consumer**: a reactive node that always listens to what it reads, as each template's consumer does; its `consumerMarkedDirty` flags the ancestors for traversal ([21 §9](../modules/21-change-detection.md#9-under-the-hood-the-notification-the-dirty-flags-and-the-refresh))
- **`allViews`**: the `ApplicationRef`'s list of attached views, each refreshed by `synchronizeOnce` and compared by the development check ([21 §9](../modules/21-change-detection.md#9-under-the-hood-the-notification-the-dirty-flags-and-the-refresh))

## Module 22

- **Observable**: a push collection of zero to many values; a cold source creates a producer per subscription, while a hot source uses a producer outside them ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **producer / consumer (RxJS)**: the producer is the function that creates an Observable's values, the consumer is the code that receives them ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **push / pull**: push: the producer decides when a value arrives; pull: the consumer asks for it ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **subscription**: the handle returned by `subscribe` that stops one run of the producer ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **lazy (Observable)**: for a cold Observable, creating it runs nothing and each `subscribe` starts a new run of the producer ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **teardown (RxJS)**: the cleanup function a producer may return, run when its subscription ends ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **microtask**: a job queued after the current code finishes, where Promise handlers run ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **`AbortController`**: the object that carries a cancel signal to an operation ([22 §1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#1-push-collections-observables-promises-iterators-and-laziness))
- **observer**: the consumer's set of callbacks, `next`, `error` and `complete`, each optional ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **notification**: one call of an observer's callbacks: a `next` with a value, an `error` or a `complete` ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **subscriber**: the internal class that wraps an observer and adds the guarantees RxJS gives: it stops after `complete` or `error` and closes its subscription ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **notification contract**: zero or more `next`, then at most one `error` or `complete`, then nothing; the teardown runs once ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **`closed`**: the flag of a subscription that has been unsubscribed or ended; `Subscription.closed` ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **`Unsubscribable`**: any object with an `unsubscribe()` method; one of the forms a producer's teardown may take ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **temporal dead zone**: the stretch of a scope before a `let` or `const` declaration runs, where reading the name throws a `ReferenceError` ([22 §2](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#2-anatomy-observable-observer-subscription-and-teardown))
- **cold (source)**: a source whose producer is created by each subscription, so it is always unicast ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **hot (source)**: a source whose producer exists outside the subscriptions, so it is almost always multicast ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **unicast / multicast**: unicast: one consumer observes a producer; multicast: many consumers observe one producer ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **`defer`**: a creation function that runs a factory at each `subscribe` and subscribes to what it returns ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **reference count (`share`)**: the number of subscribers `share` is serving; at zero it resets and unsubscribes from the source ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **backpressure**: the consumer telling the producer how much it can take; Reactor has it through `request(n)`, RxJS 7 has no such channel ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **`Subject`**: an Observable that is also an observer, so values can be pushed into it ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **`Flux`**: Project Reactor's asynchronous sequence of 0 to N items ([22 §3](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#3-cold-and-hot-unicast-and-multicast))
- **`BehaviorSubject`**: a `Subject` constructed with an initial value that gives every new subscriber the current value at once; `getValue()` reads it ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **`ReplaySubject`**: a `Subject` that stores the last n values (unbounded by default) and replays them to every new subscriber ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **`AsyncSubject`**: a `Subject` that emits only its last value, and only when it completes ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **buffer (`ReplaySubject`)**: the stored values a `ReplaySubject` replays to a late subscriber ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **`asObservable`**: returns an Observable view of a Subject that has no `next`, `error` or `complete` ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **re-entrancy**: a `next` called from inside a subscriber while a delivery is running; it finishes before the outer delivery goes on ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **`currentObservers` (internal)**: the copy of a Subject's observer list that `next` iterates ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **`AnonymousSubject`**: a `Subject` that forwards `next`, `error` and `complete` to a given observer and subscribers to a given source; `WebSocketSubject` extends it ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **`WebSocketSubject`**: the `Subject` from `rxjs/webSocket` that sends to and receives from a WebSocket ([22 §4](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#4-subjects-subject-behaviorsubject-replaysubject-and-asyncsubject))
- **creation function**: a function that returns an Observable, such as `of` or `interval` ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **pipeable operator**: a function from an Observable to an Observable that leaves its input unchanged; subscribing to the result subscribes to the input ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **operator factory**: a function that takes arguments and returns a pipeable operator, such as `map(fn)` ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **`pipe` (RxJS)**: applies operators left to right; the method `source.pipe(...)` applies them to a source, the standalone `pipe(...)` composes them into one operator ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **`OperatorFunction` / `MonoTypeOperatorFunction`**: the types of an operator, `Observable<T>` to `Observable<R>`, and the same with `R` equal to `T` ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **referentially transparent (operator)**: one operator value can be applied to many Observables without changing state in the operator ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **`rxjs/operators` subpath**: the older import site for operators; the docs call it deprecated without a version, and the 7.2.0 CHANGELOG says it "will be deprecated soon" ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **`lift` / `operate` (internal)**: the internal functions the built-in operators use to wrap a source ([22 §5](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#5-pipeable-operators-and-writing-your-own))
- **transformation / filtering / utility / conditional / aggregation (operator families)**: operators grouped by purpose: change values or shape; drop or stop; inspect or run side effects; ask about emptiness or membership; collapse to one value ([22 §6](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- **accumulator and seed**: the running result `scan` and `reduce` carry, and the optional initial value of it ([22 §6](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- **`EmptyError`**: the error `first`, `last`, `single` and `throwIfEmpty` send when the source completes without a value ([22 §6](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- **higher-order mapping**: an operator that takes a function returning an Observable, such as `switchMap` (module 23) ([22 §6](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- **`scanInternals` (internal)**: the shared function behind `scan` and `reduce` ([22 §6](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- **notifier**: an Observable used only as a signal by operators such as `buffer`, `takeUntil` and `skipUntil`; its values are ignored ([22 §6](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#6-the-operator-catalogue-creation-transformation-filtering-utility-conditional-and-aggregation))
- **marble diagram**: a text picture of a stream over virtual time, one character per frame: `-` nothing, a letter a value, `|` complete, `#` error ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **frame**: one tick of virtual time in a marble diagram; frame 0 is the first character ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **interleave**: to pass on every value from several sources in the order they arrive, as `merge` does ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **concurrency (`merge`)**: the most sources `merge` subscribes to at a time; later sources wait for a slot ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **latest value**: the last value a source has emitted so far, which `combineLatest` and `withLatestFrom` combine ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **`ObservableInput`**: anything `from` accepts as a source (an Observable, array-like, Promise, iterable, async iterable or `ReadableStream`) ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **`combineLatest`**: emits an array of the latest value of each source on every emission, once every source has emitted ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **`withLatestFrom`**: an operator that emits only when its source emits, paired with the latest values of the other inputs ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **`zip`**: pairs the n-th values of its sources, buffering the faster ones ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **`forkJoin`**: emits the last value of each source once all have completed, and nothing if one completes empty ([22 §7](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#7-combining-streams-combinelatest-withlatestfrom-forkjoin-zip-merge-and-concat))
- **burst**: values that arrive close together, followed by a pause ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **silence (`debounceTime`)**: a stretch of `d` with no new value, after which `debounceTime(d)` emits ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **debounce**: emit the last value only after a silence of `d` ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **throttle**: emit a value, then ignore the source for `d`; with `trailing` also emit the last value of the window ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **audit**: start a timer on a value and emit the latest value when it ends ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **sample**: emit the latest value at each tick of a clock, only if a new one arrived ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **leading / trailing edge**: the start and the end of a timing window ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **fake timers**: a test clock that replaces the timer functions and is advanced by hand, `vi.useFakeTimers()` ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **scheduler**: the object that decides when RxJS work runs; timing operators default to `asyncScheduler`, which uses `setInterval` (module 23) ([22 §8](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#8-timing-operators-debouncetime-throttletime-audittime-and-sampletime))
- **internal API**: code shipped in the `rxjs` package but not exported from `'rxjs'` or tagged an implementation detail; it may change in the next major ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **`SafeSubscriber` (internal)**: the subscriber `subscribe` wraps around the consumer's observer ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **`OperatorSubscriber` (internal)**: the subscriber each built-in operator adds to the chain; it turns a throw in the operator into an `error` notification ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **`ConsumerObserver` (internal)**: the wrapper inside `SafeSubscriber` that catches a consumer handler's throw and reports it later ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **destination**: the observer or subscriber a subscriber forwards notifications to ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **finalizer**: a teardown stored in a subscription's internal `_finalizers` list and run on `unsubscribe` ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **observer list**: the array of subscribers a `Subject` delivers to; `next` iterates a copy of it ([22 §9](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#9-under-the-hood-subscribe-the-subscriber-chain-and-the-subjects-observer-list))
- **idempotent (`unsubscribe`)**: a second call has no further effect ([22 Exercise 22.1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#ex22-1))
- **`Symbol.observable`**: the interop key RxJS reads on a foreign object to adopt it as an Observable (`'@@observable'` where the symbol is missing) ([22 Exercise 22.1](../modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md#ex22-1))

## Module 23

- **higher-order Observable**: an Observable whose values are Observables ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **outer / inner Observable**: the outer Observable emits values; each inner Observable is the one an operator creates and subscribes for one outer value ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **flattening operator**: an operator that maps each outer value to an inner Observable, subscribes to it and forwards its values into one stream (`switchMap`, `mergeMap`, `concatMap`, `exhaustMap`) ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **buffer (flattening)**: the queue of outer values waiting for a free inner slot in `mergeMap` with a limit and `concatMap` ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **concurrency (flattening)**: the most inner Observables subscribed at once; `mergeMap` defaults to `Infinity`, `concatMap` is 1 ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **virtual time**: the clock of `TestScheduler.run`, advanced by the test instead of the wall clock ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **`ObservableInput`**: anything `from` accepts (the 7.8.2 `types.d.ts` union: an Observable or interop Observable, a Promise-like, an array-like, an iterable, an async iterable or a `ReadableStream`-like), and so anything a flattening operator accepts as an inner ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **result selector**: the deprecated second function argument of `switchMap`, `mergeMap`, `concatMap` and `exhaustMap` that combined outer and inner values; replaced by an inner `map` ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **query / command (flattening choice)**: a query is a read whose old answer is worthless once a new one is asked (`switchMap`); a command is a write that must not run twice at once (`exhaustMap`) ([23 §2](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- **accumulator**: the running state that `scan`, `mergeScan` and `switchScan` carry from one value to the next ([23 §2](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- **race condition**: a result that depends on which async job finishes first ([23 §2](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- **idempotent (request)**: repeating it changes nothing more than doing it once ([23 §2](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- **`HttpTestingController`**: the test-side handle on the in-memory test backend; the `cancelled` flag of each `TestRequest` it returns reports that the request's subscription was torn down ([23 §2](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- **abort (a request)**: stop it on the client; `HttpClient`'s backends do it in their teardown (`aborter.abort()`, `xhr.abort()`) ([23 §2](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#2-choosing-a-flattening-operator-races-lost-writes-cancellation-and-recursion))
- **terminal notification**: `error` or `complete`; after one, a stream sends nothing more ([23 §3](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- **fallback / rethrow (`catchError`)**: the replacement a `catchError` selector returns: a substitute value or stream (fallback), or a new error (rethrow) ([23 §3](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- **`caught`**: the second argument of a `catchError` selector, the source with the same `catchError` applied; returning it subscribes again ([23 §3](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- **`finalize`**: an operator whose callback runs once when the subscription ends by complete, error or unsubscribe, after the source teardown ([23 §3](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- **`onErrorResumeNextWith`**: an operator that moves on to the next source when the current one errors or completes, so errors disappear ([23 §3](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- **error factory**: the function passed to `throwError(() => err)`, called at each subscription to build the error ([23 §3](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#3-errors-catcherror-placement-finalize-and-error-versus-complete))
- **re-subscription**: subscribing to the same source again, as if for the first time; a cold source runs again, so a retried HTTP call is a new request ([23 §4](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- **backoff / exponential backoff**: a wait before each retry; exponential backoff doubles it each time ([23 §4](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- **notifier (`retry`/`repeat` delay)**: the Observable a `delay` option yields; its first value re-subscribes, its complete ends the result without the error, its error ends it with that error ([23 §4](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- **retryable error**: a failure a later attempt may not meet (a 503), unlike one that fails the same way every time (a 400) ([23 §4](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- **jitter**: a random part added to each retry wait so that many clients do not retry in step ([23 §4](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- **`resetOnSuccess`**: the `retry` option that zeroes the retry count whenever the source emits ([23 §4](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#4-retrying-and-repeating-retry-with-backoff-and-repeat))
- **reset (`share`)**: forgetting the shared run (Subject and source subscription) so the next subscriber starts a new one; decided separately at zero subscribers, on complete and on error ([23 §5](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- **connector**: the `share`/`connect` option that creates the Subject between the source and the subscribers (`shareReplay` uses a `ReplaySubject`) ([23 §5](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- **replay buffer**: the values a `ReplaySubject` (and so `shareReplay`) keeps for late subscribers, up to `bufferSize` and `windowTime` ([23 §5](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- **stale data**: a replayed value from a run nobody was watching, delivered to a late subscriber as if current ([23 §5](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- **grace period (`share`)**: a reset notifier such as `resetOnRefCountZero: () => timer(n)` that delays the reset, so a subscriber arriving within the wait reuses the run ([23 §5](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- **`connect`**: an operator that subscribes the source once into a Subject and lets a selector build several branches over it inside one subscription ([23 §5](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#5-multicasting-in-depth-share-configuration-sharereplay-and-connect))
- **owner (of a subscription)**: the object whose lifetime a subscription serves, and whose destruction should end it ([23 §6](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- **leak (subscription)**: a subscription, and everything it references, kept alive after its owner is gone ([23 §6](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- **unsubscription strategy**: the choice of who ends a subscription: the stream (it completes) or the owner (`takeUntilDestroyed`, the `async` pipe, `toSignal`) ([23 §6](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- **`Subject.observed`**: `true` while a Subject has at least one subscriber; a test can assert it to prove a subscription ended ([23 §6](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#6-unsubscription-strategies-in-angular-and-finding-leaks))
- **state (versus event)**: a value that exists now and is read synchronously, which is what a signal holds ([23 §7](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- **event (versus state)**: something that happens at a time, with a when, a how often, a way to cancel and a way to combine, which is what an Observable describes ([23 §7](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- **conversion at the edge**: turning a stream into state once, where it enters the signal graph (`toSignal`, `rxResource`), and back into a stream only when time or cancellation is needed ([23 §7](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- **typeahead**: a search box that queries as the user types, which needs a rate limit, a change filter, cancellation of the older query and a per-query recovery ([23 §8](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- **polling**: asking again for the same data on a schedule, here one request per tick or one request a fixed delay after each response ([23 §8](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- **starvation (polling)**: no result ever arrives because every new trigger cancels the request the one before started; `switchMap` over a request slower than the tick period ([23 §8](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- **cache-then-network**: showing a stored answer first and then the fresh one, in that order ([23 §8](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- **stale-while-revalidate**: serving the stored value while a fresh one loads, and replacing it when it arrives ([23 §8](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#8-angular-patterns-typeahead-polling-cache-then-network-and-racing-requests))
- **scheduler**: the piece that decides when work runs and supplies the clock (`now()`); RxJS has `queueScheduler`, `asapScheduler`, `asyncScheduler` and `animationFrameScheduler` ([23 §9](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- **trampoline**: running work scheduled from inside running work after the current work ends, instead of nesting it, which is what `queueScheduler` does ([23 §9](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- **macrotask**: a task of the event loop, such as a `setTimeout` or `setInterval` callback; every microtask runs before the next one ([23 §9](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- **clock (scheduler)**: the `now()` a scheduler supplies, which is what virtual time replaces in tests ([23 §9](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))
- **virtual time**: a clock that jumps from one scheduled action to the next instead of waiting, which `TestScheduler.run` provides by swapping the timer providers ([23 §10](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- **time progression**: the `10ms` form of a marble diagram, which moves the virtual clock forward without an event ([23 §10](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- **subscription marble**: a marble string with `^` for subscribe and `!` for unsubscribe, asserted with `expectSubscriptions` ([23 §10](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- **flush**: running the queued virtual actions in frame order, which `run` does after the callback ([23 §10](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- **provider delegate**: an internal hook (`delegate`) through which `run` redirects the timer providers to the test scheduler ([23 §10](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#10-testing-with-testscheduler-marble-syntax-and-virtual-time-under-the-hood))
- **teardown (RxJS)**: the cleanup run when a subscription ends; `switchMap` completes before the inner's teardown, `mergeMap` after it ([23 §1](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#1-higher-order-observables-switchmap-mergemap-concatmap-and-exhaustmap))
- **`computed`**: a signal derived from others; `toSignal` returns one over its internal state signal ([23 §7](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#7-rxjs-and-signals-decision-rules-tosignal-toobservable-and-rxresource))
- **time provider (RxJS)**: an internal object wrapping a platform timer or clock (`intervalProvider`, `timeoutProvider`, `dateTimestampProvider`…) that the schedulers call; not an Angular DI provider ([23 §9](../modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md#9-schedulers-queue-asap-async-and-animation-frame-observeon-and-subscribeon))

## Module 24

- **route**: an entry of the route table, `{ path, component | children | loadChildren | redirectTo, … }` ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- **route table (`Routes`)**: the array of routes given to `provideRouter`, matched in array order ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- **router feature**: an optional `with…` function passed to `provideRouter` after the table, such as `withDebugTracing()` ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- **URL segment**: one part of the URL path between slashes, the unit the router consumes while matching ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- **path match (prefix, full)**: `'prefix'` (default) matches when the route's path is a prefix of the remaining segments; `'full'` only when it consumes all of them ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- **redirect (relative, absolute, function)**: a route's `redirectTo` rewrites the URL and matching continues; absolute when it starts with `/` or returns a `UrlTree`; a function receives a `PartialMatchRouteSnapshot` and runs in an injection context ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
