- **wildcard route**: the route with path `'**'`, which matches any remaining URL and keeps it ([24 §1](../modules/24-routing.md#1-router-configuration-providerouter-route-matching-redirects-and-the-wildcard))
- **`UrlTree`**: the parsed form of a URL: segment groups, segments with matrix parameters, one query and one fragment ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **segment group**: a node of a `UrlTree` holding the segments of one outlet (the primary group for ordinary URLs) ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **matrix parameter**: a `;key=value` pair on one URL segment, scoped to that segment ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **query parameter**: a `key=value` pair after `?`, shared by the whole URL ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **fragment**: the part after `#`, shared by the whole URL ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **path parameter**: a `:name` segment of a route path, captured from the matching URL segment ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **`ActivatedRoute` and snapshot**: the object exposing a route's params, query, fragment and data as observables, and its snapshot, which does not follow later changes ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **`paramMap`**: the route's params as a `ParamMap`, read with `get` ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **parameter inheritance**: what a child route receives of its parents' params and data, set by `paramsInheritanceStrategy` (`'always'` since v22, `'emptyOnly'` before) ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **componentless route**: a route with no `component` (or `loadComponent`), which passes its params and data to children under both strategies ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **component input binding**: `withComponentInputBinding`: the router sets the routed component's inputs from query, params, data and resolved data, and `undefined` for a missing key ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **component reuse**: the router keeps the same component instance when the next route has the same route config, so only observables and inputs see new params ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **resolver**: a function whose result becomes route data before activation ([24 §2](../modules/24-routing.md#2-route-parameters-path-matrix-query-and-fragment-inheritance-and-component-input-binding))
- **router outlet**: the `<router-outlet />` placeholder where the router inserts the component of a route; routed content follows it as a sibling ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **primary outlet**: the unnamed outlet, which shows the routes without an `outlet` property ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **named (auxiliary) outlet**: an outlet with a `name`, showing the routes marked `outlet: '<name>'`; its branch is written in parentheses in the URL and navigates on its own ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **router state**: the tree of activated routes for the current URL ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **outlet tree**: the tree of outlets and their rendered components, which mirrors the route tree ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **outlet commands**: the `{ outlets: { name: [...] | null } }` object in a `navigate` array, which changes one outlet's branch and removes it with `null` ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **`routerOutletData`**: an input of `RouterOutlet` whose value a routed component reads as a signal through `ROUTER_OUTLET_DATA` ([24 §3](../modules/24-routing.md#3-nested-and-named-outlets))
- **loader**: a route's `loadChildren` or `loadComponent` function, which returns routes or a component, usually through a dynamic `import()`; `loadChildren` runs during recognition, `loadComponent` after the resolvers, and each result is cached on the route ([24 §4](../modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- **recognition**: the step where the router matches the URL against the route table, evaluating `canMatch` guards and loading `loadChildren` routes ([24 §4](../modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- **route injector**: the environment injector the router creates once for a route that lists `providers`, and keeps unless auto cleanup destroys it ([24 §4](../modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- **auto cleanup (`withAutoCleanupInjectors`)**: the router feature that destroys the injectors of routes that are no longer active after each `NavigationEnd` ([24 §4](../modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- **`RouteConfigLoadStart` / `RouteConfigLoadEnd`**: the router events emitted around the first load of a lazy route's loader ([24 §4](../modules/24-routing.md#4-lazy-loading-in-depth-loaders-canmatch-and-route-injectors))
- **guard**: a function the router calls at one step of a navigation (`canMatch`, `canActivate`, `canActivateChild`, `canDeactivate`) to decide whether the navigation goes on ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`GuardResult`**: `boolean | UrlTree | RedirectCommand`, what a guard returns: `false` cancels, the other two redirect ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`MaybeAsync`**: a value, an Observable or a Promise of it; the router keeps the first value of an Observable or Promise guard ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`RedirectCommand`**: a `UrlTree` plus navigation options, returned or thrown by a guard to cancel and redirect ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **guard factory**: a function that returns a guard and closes over its arguments, such as `requireRole('admin')` ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`PartialMatchRouteSnapshot`**: the partial route snapshot (params, query params, URL and more) that `canMatch` and a redirect function receive ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`canLoad`**: the guard that ran before a `loadChildren` loader, deprecated in favor of `canMatch` ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`mapToCanActivate`**: a helper that turns class guards into functional guards ([24 §5](../modules/24-routing.md#5-functional-guards-canmatch-canactivate-candeactivate-and-redirectcommand))
- **`ResolveFn<T>`**: the function type of a resolver, `(route, state) => T | RedirectCommand` as a value, Promise or Observable, run in the route's injection context ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **resolved data**: the first value of each resolver, merged into the route's `data` under its `resolve` key ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **`NoDataFromResolver`**: the cancellation code of a navigation whose resolver completed without a value ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **`NavigationError`**: the router event for a navigation that failed with an error, for example a resolver that threw; the navigation promise rejects ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **navigation error handler (`withNavigationErrorHandler`)**: a function run in an injection context when a navigation fails; returning a `RedirectCommand` turns the error into a redirect ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **`runGuardsAndResolvers`**: the route property that sets when guards and resolvers run again for a route that stays active; the default `'paramsChange'` ignores query changes ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **`onSameUrlNavigation`**: the router option that sets what a navigation to the current URL does: `'ignore'` (default, skipped) or `'reload'` ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **router resource**: a `resource` returned by a route's `resources` function, enabled by `withRouterResources()`; blocking unless wrapped in `nonBlocking()` ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **blocking and non-blocking resource**: a blocking resource makes the navigation wait and binds its value; a `nonBlocking` one lets the route activate and binds the whole `Resource` ([24 §6](../modules/24-routing.md#6-resolvers-and-route-data-resolvefn-failures-and-router-resources))
- **navigation and transition**: one attempt to reach a URL, run by the router as a transition with a navigation id that grows by one per attempt ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **router event**: an object emitted on `Router.events` at each step of a navigation, from `NavigationStart` to a terminal event ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **terminal event**: the one event that ends a navigation: `NavigationEnd`, `NavigationCancel`, `NavigationError` or `NavigationSkipped` ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **`NavigationCancellationCode`**: the enum on `NavigationCancel.code`: `Redirect` 0, `SupersededByNewNavigation` 1, `NoDataFromResolver` 2, `GuardRejected` 3, `Aborted` 4 ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **superseded navigation**: a navigation cancelled with code 1 because a newer one started ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **`Navigation` object**: the description of a navigation (`id`, `extractedUrl`, `finalUrl`, `trigger`, `abort`) held by `currentNavigation` while it runs ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **`currentNavigation`**: the `Router` signal holding the running `Navigation`, or `null` when idle ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **`lastSuccessfulNavigation`**: the `Router` signal holding the `Navigation` of the last navigation that ended with `NavigationEnd`; a property before v21 ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **abort**: `Navigation.abort()`, which cancels a running navigation with code `Aborted` until activation starts ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **`isActive`**: the function `isActive(url, router, matchOptions?)` that returns a `Signal<boolean>` for whether a URL is active ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **activation**: the step that creates or reuses the components of the new router state and ends in `NavigationEnd` ([24 §7](../modules/24-routing.md#7-the-navigation-lifecycle-events-cancellation-and-the-transition-pipeline))
- **preloading**: loading a lazy route's configuration or chunk in the background before the user navigates to it ([24 §8](../modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- **preloading strategy**: a class with `preload(route, load)`, called once per lazy route, that returns `load()` to load now or `of(null)` to skip; `NoPreloading` is the default and `PreloadAllModules` loads every lazy route ([24 §8](../modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- **`PreloadAllModules`**: the built-in preloading strategy that loads every lazy route after a `NavigationEnd`, started by the router's bootstrap listener ([24 §8](../modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- **bootstrap listener**: a callback Angular runs after the root component is created; the router's one starts preloading and in-memory scrolling ([24 §8](../modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- **route reuse strategy**: the `RouteReuseStrategy` that decides whether a route keeps its component (`shouldReuseRoute`) and whether a leaving route is detached and stored and a returning one reattached ([24 §8](../modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- **detached route and `DetachedRouteHandle`**: a leaving route kept alive but out of the page and stored by a strategy as an opaque `DetachedRouteHandle`, which holds its component until the router reattaches it or `destroyDetachedRouteHandle` destroys it ([24 §8](../modules/24-routing.md#8-preloading-strategies-and-route-reuse))
- **`TitleStrategy`**: the class that turns the router state into the document title; the default sets the deepest primary route's `title` and leaves the old title when none exists ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **`Title` service**: the platform-browser service whose `setTitle` and `getTitle` write and read `document.title` ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **in-memory scrolling (`withInMemoryScrolling`)**: the router feature that scrolls after a navigation to the stored position, the top or the fragment's anchor; off by default ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **`ViewportScroller`**: the Angular service wrapping the browser's scroll calls (`scrollToPosition`, `scrollToAnchor`, `setHistoryScrollRestoration`) ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **`Scroll` event**: the router event emitted after `NavigationEnd` carrying the restored position (back or forward only) and the fragment anchor ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **anchor scrolling**: scrolling to the element named by the URL fragment, enabled with `anchorScrolling: 'enabled'` ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **View Transitions API (`startViewTransition`)**: the browser API that animates between the old and new DOM; the router calls it with `withViewTransitions` ([24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **`::view-transition-old()` / `::view-transition-new()`**: the CSS pseudo-elements for the snapshot of the old view and the live new view, styled to customize the animation (MDN; [24 §9](../modules/24-routing.md#9-view-transitions-scroll-restoration-and-titles))
- **`Location`**: the Angular service that writes and reads the app's URL through a `LocationStrategy`; `path()` returns the route without the base href ([24 §10](../modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- **`LocationStrategy`**: the class that decides how a route becomes a browser URL; `PathLocationStrategy` is the default and `HashLocationStrategy` comes from `withHashLocation()` ([24 §10](../modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- **`PlatformLocation`**: the layer under the strategies that reaches the browser's History API ([24 §10](../modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- **base href**: the URL prefix of the app, from `APP_BASE_HREF` or the document's `<base href>`, which `PathLocationStrategy` puts before every URL ([24 §10](../modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- **deep link and server fallback**: a URL that names a path inside the app, and the server rule that answers unknown paths with `index.html` so a reload reaches the Router ([24 §10](../modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))
- **`browserUrl`**: the navigation option, also a `RouterLink` input, that sets the address-bar URL apart from the routed URL ([24 §10](../modules/24-routing.md#10-the-router-and-the-platform-history-api-navigation-api-and-location-strategies))

## Module 25

- **form model**: the tree of `FormControl`, `FormGroup` and related objects that holds a form's values and states ([25 §1](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- **reactive form**: a form whose model is created in the component class and bound with `ReactiveFormsModule` directives such as `[formControl]` and `formControlName` ([25 §1](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- **template-driven form**: a form whose model is created by `FormsModule` directives such as `ngModel` and `NgForm` as the template renders ([25 §1](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- **value accessor (`ControlValueAccessor`)**: the adapter between a form control and a DOM element or custom component ([25 §1](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#1-two-forms-apis-template-driven-and-reactive-and-the-control-directive-and-accessor-triangle))
- **`AbstractControl`**: the base class of every node of a form model; `value`, `status`, `setValue`, `patchValue`, `reset` and `get` live here ([25 §2](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#2-the-reactive-model-formcontrol-formgroup-formarray-and-formrecord))
- **`FormGroup` and `FormRecord`**: a control holding named children, of a fixed shape for `FormGroup` and of one type under run-time keys for `FormRecord`, which extends it ([25 §2](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#2-the-reactive-model-formcontrol-formgroup-formarray-and-formrecord))
- **disabled control**: a control with status `DISABLED`, which leaves its parent's `value` and validation but stays in `getRawValue()` ([25 §2](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#2-the-reactive-model-formcontrol-formgroup-formarray-and-formrecord))
- **typed forms**: the generic form model classes (`FormControl<T>`, `FormGroup<{…}>`) that type `value`, `controls` and `get` from the controls they hold ([25 §3](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- **nullable control and `nonNullable`**: a control typed `T | null` because `reset()` writes `null`; `nonNullable: true` types it `T` and makes `reset()` restore the creation value ([25 §3](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- **`FormBuilder` and `NonNullableFormBuilder`**: shorthand that builds the model classes from plain values, with nullable controls for `FormBuilder` and non-nullable ones for `NonNullableFormBuilder` ([25 §3](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- **optional control key**: an optional property (`nickname?`) in a `FormGroup`'s type, the only key that `addControl` and `removeControl` accept at compile time ([25 §3](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- **`UntypedFormGroup`**: an alias of `FormGroup<any>` (and its siblings `UntypedFormControl`, `UntypedFormArray`, `UntypedFormBuilder`) that opts out of the typed checks; not marked deprecated in the typings ([25 §3](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#3-typed-forms-formbuilder-and-nonnullableformbuilder))
- **`NgForm`**: the directive that attaches to a `<form>` (unless `ngNoForm`) and owns the `FormGroup` that template-driven controls register with ([25 §4](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- **`NgModel` and the `name` attribute**: the `ngModel` directive, which creates a `FormControl` keyed by `name` and registers it with the nearest `NgForm` or `ngModelGroup` in a microtask ([25 §4](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- **`ngModelGroup`**: a directive that nests the `ngModel` controls inside it in a child group of the form ([25 §4](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- **`ngModelOptions`**: the options object of one `ngModel`; `standalone: true` keeps the control out of the enclosing form ([25 §4](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- **`ControlContainer`**: the abstract parent token that `NgForm` and `NgModelGroup` provide and `NgModel` injects with `@Host()` ([25 §4](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- **template validator directive**: a validator attached by an attribute selector (`required`, `minlength`, `email`, `min` on a number input) instead of passed as a function ([25 §4](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#4-template-driven-forms-in-depth-ngmodel-ngform-and-asynchronous-registration))
- **validator and `ValidatorFn`**: a function `(control) => ValidationErrors | null` that every control runs on each validation; `null` means valid ([25 §5](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- **error map (`ValidationErrors`)**: the object a validator returns, keyed by rule name, which the control merges with the other validators' maps into `errors` ([25 §5](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- **validator factory**: a function that returns a new validator closed over its arguments, such as `Validators.minLength(3)`, so each call returns a different function ([25 §5](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- **cross-field validator**: a validator placed on a parent group that reads several children and puts its error on the group, not on a child ([25 §5](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#5-validators-built-in-custom-and-cross-field))
- **async validator (`AsyncValidatorFn`)**: a function returning a Promise or Observable of `ValidationErrors | null` that runs only after the sync validators pass and leaves the control `PENDING` until it emits ([25 §6](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- **`PENDING`**: the control status while an async validator runs; neither `VALID` nor `INVALID` ([25 §6](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- **debounce inside a validator**: a `timer(ms)` before the request in an async validator, so a run cancelled by the next value never reaches the server ([25 §6](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- **`NG_ASYNC_VALIDATORS`**: the multi-provider token on which a directive implementing `AsyncValidator` registers ([25 §6](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#6-async-validators-pending-status-cancellation-and-debouncing))
- **status (`VALID`, `INVALID`, `PENDING`, `DISABLED`)**: the one of four strings every control carries; the booleans `valid`, `invalid`, `pending` and `disabled` each match one of them ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **pristine/dirty and touched/untouched**: two flags: dirty means the user changed the value in the view, touched means the field was blurred; a programmatic `setValue` sets neither ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **`updateOn`**: the option (`'change'`, `'blur'` or `'submit'`) that chooses which view event writes the value to the model; it gates only view-to-model ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **`valueChanges` and `statusChanges`**: the Observables of a control that emit its new value and its new status after each change, and not on subscribe ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **`events` and `ControlEvent`**: the unified stream of typed events (`ValueChangeEvent`, `StatusChangeEvent`, `PristineChangeEvent`, `TouchedChangeEvent`, `FormSubmittedEvent`, `FormResetEvent`), each with a `source`, since v18.0 ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **`emitEvent` and `onlySelf`**: the two options of the model-changing methods: `emitEvent: false` emits nothing, `onlySelf: true` leaves the parent untouched ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **`NgControlStatus`**: the directive whose host bindings set the `ng-valid`, `ng-touched`, `ng-pristine` and similar classes from the control's signal-backed state ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **internal signal (of a control)**: a signal behind the status, pristine and touched getters; reading it in a binding marks the view, and `value` has no such signal ([25 §7](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#7-status-updateon-valuechanges-and-the-unified-events-stream))
- **error display policy**: the rule that decides when a field's error shows, here `invalid` and (`touched` or `submitted`) ([25 §8](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- **error message map**: one function from error keys and their payload, such as `requiredLength`, to the text every field shows ([25 §8](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- **`submitted` and `ng-submitted`**: the flag on `NgForm` and `FormGroupDirective` set by a submit, valid or not, and the class the `<form>` gets from it ([25 §8](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- **`aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`**: attributes a template binds from the control's state so the input is marked invalid and points at its message ([25 §8](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#8-error-display-strategies-and-accessible-errors))
- **`formArrayName` and `[formArray]`**: two directives: the first links a nested `FormArray` by name, the second binds a root `FormArray` to an element ([25 §9](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- **dynamic form**: a form whose shape is known only at run time, such as repeatable rows ([25 §9](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- **config-driven form**: a dynamic form whose controls and fields are built from data, such as a server's JSON config ([25 §9](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- **field registry**: a strategy per field type, a lookup from a field's `type` to the template or component that renders it ([25 §9](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#9-dynamic-forms-formarray-patterns-and-config-driven-forms))
- **`ControlValueAccessor` (CVA)**: the adapter between a control and a view: the form directive calls `writeValue` (model to view) and `setDisabledState`, and gives it the `registerOnChange` and `registerOnTouched` callbacks (view to model) ([25 §10](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- **`NG_VALUE_ACCESSOR`**: the multi-provider token under which a component or directive registers itself as an accessor ([25 §10](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- **built-in accessor**: an accessor Angular ships and matches by selector on a native element, such as `NumberValueAccessor` or `CheckboxControlValueAccessor` ([25 §10](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- **`Validator` and `NG_VALIDATORS`**: the interface whose `validate(control)` returns an error map or `null`, and the multi-provider token that registers it on a control ([25 §10](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))
- **`callSetDisabledState`**: the `withConfig` option choosing whether `setDisabledState` is called on every attach (`'always'`, the default since v15) or only for a disabled control (`'whenDisabledForLegacyCode'`) ([25 §10](../modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md#10-controlvalueaccessor-and-the-validator-interface-custom-controls-under-the-hood))

## Module 26

- **Signal Forms**: the third Angular forms API (`@angular/forms/signals`, stable since 22.0): a form built from a writable model signal with `form()` ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **model signal**: the writable signal you own that holds a form's data and is the source of truth for `form()` ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **model-first form**: a form whose source of truth is a model signal, not a tree of controls ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **field tree**: the object `form()` returns, shaped like the model, whose nodes expose each value and its field state as signals ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **field state**: the signals a field exposes next to its value: errors, touched, dirty, pending and the rest ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **schema and rule**: a rule is a validation or state declaration on a path of the model; a schema groups rules, declared once per model ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **`@angular/forms/signals/compat`**: the entry point with the adapters between Signal Forms and reactive forms (`compatForm`, `SignalFormControl`) ([26 §1](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#1-why-a-third-forms-api-model-first-signals-end-to-end))
- **`form()`**: the function that wraps a writable model signal and returns its field tree; it needs an injection context or the `injector` option ([26 §2](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- **field**: a node of the field tree, a function you call to get its `FieldState`; its properties are the child fields ([26 §2](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- **`FieldState`**: the object a field returns: `value` (writable) and the signals for errors, validity, touched, dirty and the rest, with `markAsTouched()`, `markAsDirty()`, `reset()` and `getError()` ([26 §2](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- **`errorSummary`**: the field state signal listing the errors of the field and all its descendants, while `errors()` holds only the field's own ([26 §2](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#2-form-the-field-tree-and-field-state)).
- **`FormField`**: the directive with selector `[formField]` that binds one field to one native or custom control, writing both ways and reporting blur as touched ([26 §3](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- **native control**: an `<input>`, `<select>` or `<textarea>` element bound with `[formField]`, as opposed to a custom control ([26 §3](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- **status classes**: the `ng-valid`, `ng-dirty` style classes that reactive and template-driven forms add on their own and Signal Forms adds only when configured ([26 §3](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- **`provideSignalFormsConfig`**: the provider function that sets app-wide Signal Forms options, such as the `classes` map ([26 §3](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- **`NG_STATUS_CLASSES`**: the preset, exported from `@angular/forms/signals/compat`, that maps the seven `ng-` status classes to field state ([26 §3](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#3-binding-with-formfield-native-inputs-attributes-classes-and-rendering)).
- **`SchemaPath`**: the handle a schema function receives for a place in the model; rules attach to it and it holds no value ([26 §4](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- **`validate`**: the rule function that takes a path and a function returning an error, a list of errors or nothing, and reruns when the signals it reads change ([26 §4](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- **`ValidationError`**: the object a rule reports, with a `kind`, an optional `message` and rule parameters such as `min` ([26 §4](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- **`ValidationResult`**: what a `validate` function returns: `null`, `undefined` or `void` for no error, or one error or a list ([26 §4](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- **`schema()` and `apply`**: `schema()` names a reusable, non-reactive function that attaches rules, and `apply(path, schema)` mounts it on a sub-path ([26 §4](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- **Standard Schema**: the shared `~standard` object interface that validation libraries implement; `validateStandardSchema` turns its issues into field errors ([26 §4](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#4-schema-rules-and-validation-built-in-rules-validate-and-reusable-schemas)).
- **`FieldContext`**: the object a rule receives, with `value`, `valueOf(path)`, `stateOf(path)` and `fieldTree`, so a rule can read other fields ([26 §5](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- **`valueOf`**: the context function that reads another field's value inside a rule, so the rule reruns when it changes ([26 §5](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- **`validateTree`**: a rule that returns errors carrying a target `fieldTree`, which decides which field shows each error ([26 §5](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- **`applyWhen`**: mounts a schema on a path only while a condition holds ([26 §5](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- **`disabled`, `hidden` and `readonly`**: rules that take `{ when }`; a field in any of those states is skipped by validation ([26 §5](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- **`disabledReasons`**: the list of reasons a field is disabled, one entry per disabling rule ([26 §5](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#5-cross-field-and-conditional-logic-valueof-validatetree-applywhen-disabled-hidden-and-readonly)).
- **async rule**: a validation whose verdict arrives later; it runs only when the sync rules pass and leaves the field pending while it loads ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`validateAsync`**: attaches a `resource` to a field; `params`, `factory`, `onSuccess` and `onError` (plus optional `debounce` and `when`) describe it ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`validateHttp`**: `validateAsync` with an `httpResource` as the factory; `request` returns a URL or a request object ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`pending()`**: true while an async rule loads; `valid()` and `invalid()` are both false then ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`debounce()` rule**: delays model writes from the UI by milliseconds, until blur or by a custom `Debouncer`; touching the field flushes it ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`Debouncer`**: a function `(context, abortSignal)` returning a promise to hold a UI write, or nothing to apply it at once ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`reloadValidation()`**: reloads every async rule of a field and its descendants ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`onSuccess` / `onError`**: the `validateAsync` options that map a resolved result and a failure to field errors ([26 §6](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#6-async-validation-and-debounce-validateasync-validatehttp-pending-and-cancellation)).
- **`submit()`**: the function exported by `@angular/forms/signals` that marks fields touched, checks validity, runs an async action while `submitting()` is true and attaches the errors the action returns ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **submission action**: the async function `submit()` runs on a valid form; it returns nothing for success or errors that name their target `fieldTree` ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **`FormRoot`**: the `form[formRoot]` directive that sets `novalidate`, prevents the native submit and calls `submit()` when the form defines `submission` options ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **`submitting()`**: the field state signal that is `true` while a submission action runs ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **submission error**: an error returned by a submission action and attached to its target field; it clears when the field's value changes ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **`onInvalid`**: the `submit()` option called when validation blocks the action, after the interactive fields are touched ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **`ignoreValidators`**: the `submit()` option that decides which validator states block the action: `'pending'` (default), `'none'` or `'all'` ([26 §7](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#7-submission-submit-formroot-submitting-and-server-errors)).
- **custom control**: a component with one required `model()` (`value` or `checked`) that `[formField]` binds directly ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`FormValueControl`**: the contract for a control that edits a value: a required `value` model and a `checked` that must stay undefined ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`FormCheckboxControl`**: the contract for a boolean control: a required `checked` model and a `value` that must stay undefined ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`FormUiControl`**: the base interface whose optional inputs and `touch` output `[formField]` binds when a control declares them ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`touch` output**: the output a custom control emits on blur to mark its field touched ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`FORM_FIELD`**: the injection token the `FormField` directive provides ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`InteropNgControl`** (internal): the fake `NgControl` that `FormField` provides for accessor interop ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`ngNoCva`**: an attribute that opts a host out of the built-in `ControlValueAccessor` directives so a custom control binds ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`transformedValue`**: a function returning a writable raw signal synchronized with a model through `parse` and `format`, with `parseErrors()` ([26 §8](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#8-custom-controls-formvaluecontrol-formcheckboxcontrol-and-how-formfield-picks-a-control)).
- **`compatForm`**: the function in `@angular/forms/signals/compat` that builds a form from a signal model that may contain `FormControl` and `FormGroup` instances, exposing them as fields ([26 §9](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- **`SignalFormControl`**: an `AbstractControl` created from a raw value and a schema, backed by a field tree (`fieldTree`, `sourceValue`), so a `FormGroup` can contain it ([26 §9](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- **top-down migration**: moving to Signal Forms by keeping existing `FormControl` instances inside a signal model with `compatForm` ([26 §9](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- **bottom-up migration**: moving to Signal Forms by placing `SignalFormControl` leaves inside an existing `FormGroup` ([26 §9](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- **`extractValue`**: a compat function that unwraps a field tree, controls included, into its raw value, optionally filtered by dirty, touched or enabled ([26 §9](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).
- **`CompatValidationError`**: the error class that wraps a reactive control's error key and context as a signal-forms error of that `kind` ([26 §9](../modules/26-signal-forms.md#9-interop-and-migration-compatform-signalformcontrol-and-what-changed-from-v21-to-v22)).

## Module 27

- **`HttpClient`**: Angular's HTTP service (`@angular/common/http`): a typed request goes through interceptors to a backend and returns a cold Observable of events ([27 §1](../modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- **backend (`HttpBackend`)**: the implementation at the end of the interceptor chain that makes the real call; `FetchBackend` is the default in v22 and `HttpXhrBackend` uses `XMLHttpRequest` ([27 §1](../modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- **interceptor**: a function that can read or replace a request and its response as it passes through the `HttpClient` chain ([27 §1](../modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- **`HttpErrorResponse`**: the error `HttpClient` emits for a non-2xx status or a failed call ([27 §1](../modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- **`AbortSignal`**: the platform's cancellation token; the fetch backend passes one to `fetch` and aborts it when you unsubscribe ([27 §1](../modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- **`httpResource`**: the signal-driven read built on `HttpClient`, stable since v22.0 ([27 §1](../modules/27-http-client.md#1-httpclient-versus-fetch-what-the-framework-adds-over-the-platform))
- **`provideHttpClient`**: the function that registers `HttpClient`, its interceptor handler, a backend and the XSRF interceptor, then the providers of the features passed to it ([27 §2](../modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- **HTTP feature (`HttpFeature`)**: an argument of `provideHttpClient` such as `withInterceptors` or `withXhr` that contributes extra providers ([27 §2](../modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- **`withXhr`**: the feature that makes `HttpClient` use `XMLHttpRequest` through `HttpXhrBackend`, added in v22.0 ([27 §2](../modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- **`withRequestsMadeViaParent`**: the feature that sends a child injector's requests through its parent's `HttpClient` after the child's own interceptors ([27 §2](../modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- **`HttpClientModule`**: the deprecated NgModule form of the setup; its providers are `provideHttpClient(withInterceptorsFromDi(), withXhr())` ([27 §2](../modules/27-http-client.md#2-setting-up-httpclient-providehttpclient-its-features-and-the-fetch-backend-default))
- **`HttpRequest`**: the immutable object a call builds from the method, URL, body and request options; `clone` returns a changed copy ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`observe`**: the request option that picks what the Observable emits: the body, the `HttpResponse` or every event ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`responseType`**: the request option that picks how the body is parsed: `json`, `text`, `blob` or `arraybuffer` ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpResponse`**: the full response event with status, headers and body, emitted by `observe: 'response'` ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpEventType`**: the enum that types each event of an `observe: 'events'` stream, from `Sent` to `Response` ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpParams`**: the immutable URL query-parameter collection; `set`, `append` and `delete` return a new instance ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpHeaders`**: the immutable header collection; lookups are case-insensitive and changes return a new instance ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpContext`**: a per-request, Map-backed bag of metadata that interceptors read and that is not sent to the server ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpContextToken`**: the typed key of an `HttpContext` entry, with a default-value factory ([27 §3](../modules/27-http-client.md#3-making-requests-typed-responses-observe-responsetype-immutable-params-and-headers-and-httpcontext))
- **`HttpInterceptorFn`**: the function type `(req, next) => Observable<HttpEvent>` of a functional interceptor, listed with `withInterceptors` ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **`HttpHandlerFn` (`next`)**: the function an interceptor calls to run the rest of the chain; not calling it short-circuits the request ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **`withInterceptors`**: the HTTP feature that lists functional interceptors; the first listed runs first on the way out ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **short-circuit (interceptor)**: returning a response from an interceptor without calling `next`, so the backend is never reached ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **`HttpInterceptor`**: the legacy class form of an interceptor, registered under `HTTP_INTERCEPTORS` and run only with `withInterceptorsFromDi()` ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **`HTTP_INTERCEPTORS`**: the multi-provider token under which class interceptors are registered ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **`withInterceptorsFromDi`**: the HTTP feature that runs all `HTTP_INTERCEPTORS` class interceptors as one block at its position in the chain ([27 §4](../modules/27-http-client.md#4-interceptors-the-functional-chain-order-and-next-and-class-interceptors-through-withinterceptorsfromdi))
- **`HttpErrorResponse`**: the error every failed `HttpClient` request ends with: a `status`, the parsed body or thrown error in `error`, and a `message` ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **status 0**: the `status` of an `HttpErrorResponse` when no HTTP response was read: a network failure, a timeout or an aborted call ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **error mapping layer**: one function at the edge that turns an `HttpErrorResponse` into a small union of domain errors the UI renders ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **domain error**: an application-level failure kind (offline, timeout, not found, validation, server) that hides HTTP details from the UI ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **retryable error**: a failure worth another attempt: status 0, 429 or a 5xx ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **jitter**: a random share added to a retry delay so clients that failed together do not retry together ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **idempotency key**: a client-chosen unique value sent with a request so the server applies it once however often it is retried ([27 §5](../modules/27-http-client.md#5-errors-httperrorresponse-an-error-mapping-layer-and-retry-with-backoff-and-jitter))
- **cancellation (request)**: stopping a request nobody wants any more; on the fetch backend, unsubscribing aborts it ([27 §6](../modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- **cache interceptor**: an interceptor that answers repeated GETs from memory, keyed by URL, so the backend is skipped on a hit ([27 §6](../modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- **time to live (TTL)**: how long a cache entry may be served before it counts as expired ([27 §6](../modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- **cache invalidation**: dropping cache entries that a write has made stale ([27 §6](../modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- **in-flight sharing**: giving concurrent callers of the same request one backend request instead of one each ([27 §6](../modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- **transfer cache**: the server's `HttpClient` responses serialized into the HTML so the browser reuses them during hydration instead of requesting again ([27 §6](../modules/27-http-client.md#6-cancellation-and-caching-unsubscribing-aborts-cache-interceptors-sharereplay-and-the-ssr-transfer-cache))
- **bearer token**: a credential sent as `Authorization: Bearer <token>`; whoever holds it is served ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **refresh token**: a longer-lived credential exchanged for a new bearer token when the current one expires ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **auth interceptor**: an interceptor that adds the credential to requests for the API's origin and reacts to a 401 ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **single flight**: keeping one refresh in flight so concurrent callers wait on it instead of starting their own ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **request queue**: the requests waiting on a refresh and retried when it ends ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **refresh loop**: a failing refresh that triggers another refresh, so the calls never end ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **`SKIP_AUTH`**: an `HttpContextToken` that marks the refresh request so the auth interceptor leaves it alone ([27 §7](../modules/27-http-client.md#7-authentication-attaching-tokens-refreshing-on-401-with-a-request-queue-and-avoiding-refresh-loops))
- **XSRF (cross-site request forgery, also CSRF)**: an attack in which another site makes the victim's browser send a state-changing request with the victim's cookies; depth in 33 ([27 §8](../modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- **cookie-to-header token**: a defense in which the server sets a script-readable cookie and the client copies it into a header the server compares ([27 §8](../modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- **`withXsrfConfiguration`**: the HTTP feature that renames the XSRF cookie and header ([27 §8](../modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- **`withNoXsrfProtection`**: the HTTP feature that turns the built-in XSRF interceptor off ([27 §8](../modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- **`HttpXsrfTokenExtractor`**: the public abstract class that supplies the XSRF token; its default reads the cookie ([27 §8](../modules/27-http-client.md#8-xsrf-protection-how-httpclient-reads-the-cookie-and-sets-the-header-and-when-it-does-not))
- **progress event**: a request event that reports how many bytes of the request or response body have moved; `observe: 'events'` exposes it ([27 §9](../modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- **`reportUploadProgress`**: the request option that asks for `UploadProgress` events; the fetch backend throws `NG02824` when it is set, and it needs `withXhr()`; added in v22.0 ([27 §9](../modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- **`reportDownloadProgress`**: the request option that asks for `DownloadProgress` events; both backends support it; added in v22.0 ([27 §9](../modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- **`HttpUploadProgressEvent` and `HttpDownloadProgressEvent`**: the event types of request-body and response-body progress, each with `loaded` and an optional `total` ([27 §9](../modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- **`NG02824`**: the runtime error code of `FetchBackend` when upload progress is requested ([27 §9](../modules/27-http-client.md#9-progress-events-reportuploadprogress-reportdownloadprogress-and-why-upload-progress-needs-xhr))
- **`httpResource`**: the signal-driven read built on `HttpClient`: it requests eagerly, cancels the previous request when its signals change and exposes status, value and error signals ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`HttpResourceRef`**: the resource type that `httpResource` returns; it adds the `headers`, `statusCode` and `progress` signals to a writable resource ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`HttpResourceRequest`**: the request object a resource's request function may return instead of a URL; it mirrors the `HttpClient` request options ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`parse` (`httpResource` option)**: the function that turns the raw response into the resource's value; a throw puts the resource in `'error'` ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`defaultValue` (`httpResource` option)**: the value a resource reports while it has none, for example while loading ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`statusCode` (resource signal)**: the signal of the latest HTTP status of the resource's response, including an error response ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`progress` (resource signal)**: the signal of the latest download progress event, set when the request asks for progress ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **eager request**: a request sent as soon as its inputs exist, with no subscriber; `httpResource` is eager and `HttpClient` is lazy ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`httpResource.text`**: the `httpResource` variant that reads the body as a string; `blob` and `arrayBuffer` read the other body types ([27 §10](../modules/27-http-client.md#10-httpresource-signal-driven-requests-and-when-to-prefer-it-over-httpclient))
- **`provideHttpClientTesting`**: the provider function that swaps `HttpBackend` for the in-memory test backend and provides `HttpTestingController`; it goes after `provideHttpClient` ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **test backend**: the in-memory `HttpBackend` of `provideHttpClientTesting`; it holds each request open until the test answers it and simulates no `timeout` or abort ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`HttpTestingController`**: the test-side handle on the test backend, used to find, answer and verify requests ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`TestRequest`**: one open request in the test backend, answered with `flush`, `error` or `event` ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`expectOne`**: the controller method that returns the one open request matching a URL, object or predicate and throws on none or many ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`match` (controller)**: the controller method that removes and returns every open request a URL, object or predicate selects ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`expectNone`**: the controller method that throws when any open request matches ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`flush` (HTTP testing)**: the `TestRequest` method that answers with a body and status, as a response for 2xx and an `HttpErrorResponse` otherwise ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`verify`**: the controller method that throws when requests are still open; `ignoreCancelled` skips cancelled ones ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`cancelled` (`TestRequest`)**: `true` once the subscriber unsubscribed from the request ([27 §11](../modules/27-http-client.md#11-testing-and-the-backend-seam-providehttpclienttesting-httptestingcontroller-and-what-they-replace))
- **`ApiError`**: the exercise's discriminated union of failures (`invalid-response`, `not-found`, `offline`, `validation`, `unknown`) that a service returns in place of an `HttpErrorResponse` ([27 Exercise 27.1](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-1))
- **equal jitter**: a retry delay of half the plain backoff plus a random share of the other half, so it stays between half and all of the backoff ([27 Exercise 27.2](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-2))
- **`CACHE_CLOCK`**: the exercise's injected function that returns the current time in milliseconds, so cache expiry needs no real time ([27 Exercise 27.3](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-3))
- **cache entry (in-flight sharing)**: the exercise's `Map` value that holds an Observable replayed to every caller of one key, so callers that overlap share one backend request ([27 Exercise 27.3](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-3))
- **`API_ORIGIN`**: the exercise's injection token for the one origin whose requests carry the bearer token; a relative URL counts as on it ([27 Exercise 27.4](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-4))
- **`UploadState`**: the exercise's union of `progress` (a rounded-down percent), `done` (the server id) and `failed` (the error) that an upload Observable emits instead of raw events ([27 Exercise 27.5](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-5))
- **`SearchResult`**: the exercise's result row (`id`, `title`) whose hand-written guard is passed to `httpResource` as `parse`, so a wrong shape puts the resource in the error state ([27 Exercise 27.6](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-6))
- **empty state**: the template branch for a resolved resource whose value is an empty list, distinct from idle, loading and error ([27 Exercise 27.6](../modules/27-http-client.md#ex27-6))

## Module 28

- **state**: the data a screen depends on and that changes while the app runs ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **source of truth**: the one place whose value wins when copies of the same data disagree ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **server cache**: a copy of data the server owns, fetched, kept for a while and invalidated; not client state ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **client state**: state the client owns, such as UI, form, URL and session state; everything except the server cache ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **UI state**: state local to one component or feature, such as open panels and selection ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **form state**: a draft with validity, owned by the forms API ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **URL state**: state that must survive a reload and be shareable, kept in route and query parameters ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **session state**: user, tokens and preferences for this tab or device, held in a root service and optionally mirrored to storage ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **derived state**: state computed from other state and never stored ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **store**: a container for some of the kinds of state, not a kind of state itself ([28 §1](../modules/28-state-management.md#1-kinds-of-state-server-cache-ui-form-url-and-session-state-and-where-each-belongs))
- **feature store**: one service that owns a typed state in a private signal, exposes read-only selectors and changes the state only through methods ([28 §2](../modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- **selector (signal store)**: a `computed` over a store's state that components read; it re-runs after every write to the state it reads, and its dependants stop when its result is equal ([28 §2](../modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- **action method**: a store method named after intent (`search`) that is the only writer of the store's state ([28 §2](../modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- **status union**: a union of literal strings (`idle`, `loading`, `loaded`, `error`) naming the phase of an async load ([28 §2](../modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- **stale response**: a response that arrives after a newer request has replaced the query it answered ([28 §2](../modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- **single source of truth**: the one state signal of a feature store from which every selector derives, so no value is stored twice ([28 §2](../modules/28-state-management.md#2-the-service-with-signals-store-state-shape-derived-selectors-methods-and-async-loads))
- **`BehaviorSubject` store**: a service that keeps its state in a private `BehaviorSubject`, exposes a stream and `select`, and changes state with `next` of a new object ([28 §3](../modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- **`select` (store)**: a method that turns a state-picking function into a stream built from `map` and `distinctUntilChanged` ([28 §3](../modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- **`distinctUntilChanged`**: an operator that drops a value equal to the previous one; the default comparison is `===`, so objects and arrays compare by reference ([28 §3](../modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- **`async` pipe**: a template pipe that subscribes to an Observable, renders its latest value, marks the view for check on each value and unsubscribes at destroy ([28 §3](../modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- **`getValue()`**: the synchronous read of a `BehaviorSubject`'s current value; it notifies no view ([28 §3](../modules/28-state-management.md#3-the-service-with-behaviorsubject-store-the-legacy-but-common-pattern-and-its-migration))
- **Redux pattern** (unidirectional data flow): state changes in one direction, only when an action is dispatched and a reducer computes the next state ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **action**: a plain object whose string `type` says what happened, written `[Source] Event`, plus optional payload ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **action creator**: a function that builds an action object ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **`props`** and **`emptyProps`**: NgRx helpers that declare an action's payload type, or no payload ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **action group**: a `createActionGroup` call that creates one action creator per event of one source, with camelCase names and `[Source] Event` types ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **reducer**: a pure function `(state, action) => nextState` that changes state only by returning a new object ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **pure function**: a function with no side effects whose result depends only on its inputs ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **`on`**: the helper that pairs an action creator with a reducer handler inside `createReducer` ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **NgRx Store**: the single state tree, fed by dispatched actions and read through selectors ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **`provideStore`** and **`provideState`**: standalone provider functions that register the root store and a feature's reducer ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **feature** (NgRx): one named slice of the store's state tree; `createFeature` returns its name, reducer and one selector per state key ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **runtime checks**: guards around every reducer, on by default only in development for immutability ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **meta-reducer**: a function that takes a reducer and returns a wrapped reducer; the runtime checks are meta-reducers ([28 §4](../modules/28-state-management.md#4-ngrx-store-actions-reducers-createactiongroup-createfeature-and-runtime-checks))
- **selector (NgRx store)**: a memoized pure function from the state tree to a value, built with `createSelector` or `createFeatureSelector` ([28 §5](../modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- **memoization**: remembering a function's last arguments and result so equal arguments return the stored result; NgRx selectors keep one entry and compare with `===` ([28 §5](../modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- **input selector** and **projector**: the selectors that pick slices for `createSelector`, and the function that turns their results into the answer ([28 §5](../modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- **selector factory**: a function such as `(id) => createSelector(...)` that returns one selector (one memo) per argument; it replaces selectors with props ([28 §5](../modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- **`selectSignal`**: `Store.selectSignal(selector)`, a `computed` over the store's state signal ([28 §5](../modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- **`MockStore`**: the test double from `@ngrx/store/testing` whose `overrideSelector` fixes a selector's result ([28 §5](../modules/28-state-management.md#5-selectors-memoization-composition-and-reading-the-store-as-signals))
- **effect (NgRx)**: a long-lived stream over `Actions` that runs side effects and usually answers with new actions ([28 §6](../modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- **`Actions`** and **`ofType`**: the injectable stream of actions after their reducer ran, and the operator that narrows it to given action creators ([28 §6](../modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- **functional effect**: an effect written as a constant with `createEffect(fn, { functional: true })` and registered with `provideEffects({ … })` ([28 §6](../modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- **`dispatch: false`**: the `createEffect` option for an effect that emits no action ([28 §6](../modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- **effects error handler**: the function NgRx wraps around each effect; the default reports the error to `ErrorHandler` and resubscribes, up to ten times ([28 §6](../modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- **`tapResponse`** and **`mapResponse`**: `@ngrx/operators` functions that handle a response with `{ next, error }` callbacks and keep the outer stream alive ([28 §6](../modules/28-state-management.md#6-effects-functional-effects-flattening-error-handling-and-dispatch-false))
- **entity** and **normalized state**: a record with a unique id, and a collection stored once as a list of ids plus a lookup by id; `EntityState<T>` is `{ ids, entities }` ([28 §7](../modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- **entity adapter**: the pure functions from `createEntityAdapter` (`addMany`, `updateOne`, `getSelectors()`) over `EntityState`; `selectId` reads an id and `sortComparer` orders `ids` ([28 §7](../modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- **Router Store** and **router state serializer**: `@ngrx/router-store` mirrors navigation into the store as actions and state, and the serializer reduces Angular's router snapshot to a plain object ([28 §7](../modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
- **Redux DevTools**, **time travel**, **`maxAge`** and **`logOnly`**: the browser extension that `@ngrx/store-devtools` connects to, jumping to an earlier recorded state, the cap on recorded history, and the option that restricts the extension's features ([28 §7](../modules/28-state-management.md#7-ngrx-entity-router-store-and-store-devtools))
