- **`require-trusted-types-for`**: the CSP directive (`'script'`) that makes every sink throw on a plain string ([33 §6](../modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- **`trusted-types` allowlist**: the CSP directive naming the policies a page may create; creating any other throws ([33 §6](../modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- **report-only rollout**: sending the Trusted Types directives as `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` first, so each report finds a sink before enforcement breaks it ([33 §6](../modules/33-security.md#6-trusted-types-with-angular-the-frameworks-policies-enforcement-and-rollout))
- **protocol-relative URL**: a URL starting `//host/path`, which keeps the page's scheme and names another host ([33 §7](../modules/33-security.md#7-server-rendered-pages-escaping-in-the-server-html-the-state-script-and-ssrf))
- **egress allowlist**: the destinations a server is allowed to call, checked in code and by network rules; the defense against SSRF that Angular does not provide ([33 §7](../modules/33-security.md#7-server-rendered-pages-escaping-in-the-server-html-the-state-script-and-ssrf))
- **access token**: the credential an API accepts on each request, usually sent as `Authorization: Bearer <token>` ([33 §8](../modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))
- **JWT (JSON Web Token)**: a token format of claims as base64url text, usually signed; anyone holding a signed JWT can read its claims ([33 §8](../modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))
- **exfiltration**: copying a secret out of the page to a place the attacker controls ([33 §8](../modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))
- **session cookie**: a cookie holding an id the server maps to a signed-in user ([33 §8](../modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))
- **backend-for-frontend (BFF)**: a server on the app's own site that keeps the tokens and gives the browser only a session cookie ([33 §8](../modules/33-security.md#8-where-the-token-lives-memory-web-storage-httponly-cookies-and-a-backend-for-frontend))
- **CSRF (cross-site request forgery)**: another site making the user's browser send a state-changing request to your site, with your cookies attached ([33 §9](../modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- **ambient credential**: a credential the browser attaches by itself, such as a cookie, HTTP authentication or a client certificate; an `Authorization` header set by script is not ambient ([33 §9](../modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- **Fetch Metadata**: request headers the browser sets (`Sec-Fetch-Site`, `Sec-Fetch-Mode`, `Sec-Fetch-Dest`) that tell the server where a request came from ([33 §9](../modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- **`Sec-Fetch-Site`**: the Fetch Metadata header whose value is `same-origin`, `same-site`, `none` or `cross-site` ([33 §9](../modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- **`Origin` check**: the server comparing a request's `Origin` header with the app's own and rejecting a foreign one on state changes ([33 §9](../modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- **XSSI (cross-site script inclusion)**: another site loading your JSON API in a `<script>` tag; servers prefix JSON with `)]}',` and `HttpClient` strips it ([33 §9](../modules/33-security.md#9-csrf-in-single-page-apps-when-cookies-make-you-vulnerable-samesite-and-angulars-xsrf-support))
- **authentication**: establishing who the caller is; a request with no valid identity is answered `401` ([33 §10](../modules/33-security.md#10-route-guards-are-ux-authorization-belongs-on-the-server))
- **authorization**: deciding whether a known caller may do this; a refusal is answered `403`, and only the server can enforce it ([33 §10](../modules/33-security.md#10-route-guards-are-ux-authorization-belongs-on-the-server))
- **client-side gating**: hiding a route, button or field in the browser so the page offers less; it shapes the user experience and does not limit what a request can do ([33 §10](../modules/33-security.md#10-route-guards-are-ux-authorization-belongs-on-the-server))
- **lockfile**: `package-lock.json`: the exact version, tarball URL and integrity hash of every installed package, so an install repeats and a swapped file is refused ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **integrity hash**: a digest of a file or tarball (`sha512` in a lockfile, `sha384` in an SRI attribute) that changes when one byte changes ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **`npm ci`**: the clean install that uses exactly the lockfile, exits with an error when it disagrees with `package.json` and never rewrites it ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **install script**: a `preinstall`, `install` or `postinstall` script that npm runs during install; `ignore-scripts` stops it ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **release-age delay**: an npm setting (`min-release-age`, `before`) that installs only versions published long enough ago ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **advisory**: a published report that a package version is vulnerable or malicious, which `npm audit` matches against your dependencies ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **provenance**: a signed statement of where and how a package was built, which `npm audit signatures` verifies ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **Subresource Integrity (SRI)**: the browser compares a fetched script or stylesheet with the hash in its `integrity` attribute and refuses a mismatch; Angular's `subresourceIntegrity` build option writes the attributes and an import map for lazy chunks ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **transitive dependency**: a package your dependencies need, which you did not choose and a lockfile pins ([33 §11](../modules/33-security.md#11-dependency-and-supply-chain-security-lockfiles-npm-audit-install-scripts-and-subresource-integrity))
- **security schema**: the compiler's table (`SECURITY_SCHEMA`, internal) from an element and property to a security context, looked up once per binding at compile time ([33 §12](../modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- **sanitizer function reference**: the `ɵɵsanitize*` function (internal) the compiler passes as a binding instruction's last argument, called each time the bound value changes ([33 §12](../modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- **inert document**: a separate in-memory DOM (from `DOMParser`) where parsed scripts and event handlers do not run, and where Angular's HTML sanitizer inspects markup (images may still load) ([33 §12](../modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- **DOM clobbering**: markup whose `name` or `id` shadows a DOM property (an `<input name="nextSibling">` inside a `form`), which Angular's HTML sanitizer detects and refuses ([33 §12](../modules/33-security.md#12-under-the-hood-from-a-template-binding-to-a-sanitized-dom-write))
- **edge function**: code a CDN runs on each response before it reaches the visitor, for example to write a fresh nonce into cached HTML ([Q33.15](../modules/33-security.md#q33-15))

## Module 34

- **internationalization (i18n)**: designing and preparing an app so it can be adapted to different locales: no text, format or direction hard-coded ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **localization (l10n)**: building and shipping a version of an app for one locale ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **locale**: a language, optionally with a script and a region, that decides text, formats, plural rules, collation and direction; written as a BCP 47 language tag ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **BCP 47 language tag**: the IETF format for naming a language (RFC 5646), such as `fr`, `fr-CA` or `zh-Hant-TW`; case-insensitive, with a canonical form ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **subtag**: one part of a language tag: the language (required), an optional script and an optional region ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **source locale**: the locale an app's templates and code are written in; `en-US` unless `i18n.sourceLocale` sets another ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **`LOCALE_ID`**: Angular's injection token for the app's locale; a provider wins, else `$localize.locale`, else `en-US` ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **CLDR**: the Unicode Common Locale Data Repository, the data behind date, number and unit formats and collation ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **ICU (library)**: International Components for Unicode, the C/C++ and Java libraries that JavaScript engines such as Node's build `Intl` on; not ICU message syntax ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **collation**: a locale's rules for sorting text ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **`$localize`**: the global tag function from `@angular/localize` that translated text compiles to; its `locale` property holds the build's locale ([34 §1](../modules/34-i18n.md#1-i18n-l10n-and-the-locale-language-tags-what-a-locale-decides-and-locale_id))
- **formatter (`Intl`)**: an object built once from a locale and options that formats, selects or compares values, such as `Intl.DateTimeFormat` or `Intl.Collator` ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **plural category**: the label `zero`, `one`, `two`, `few`, `many` or `other` that a language's plural rule gives a number; the names are labels, not quantities ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **cardinal and ordinal plurals**: cardinal rules count things ("1 file"), ordinal rules rank them ("1st"); `Intl.PluralRules` takes `type: 'ordinal'` for the second ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **time zone (IANA name)**: a region's clock rule named like `America/New_York`; `Intl.DateTimeFormat`'s `timeZone` option takes it, and without it the runtime's zone decides ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **numbering system**: the set of digits a locale writes numbers with, such as `latn` (0-9) or `arab` (Arabic-Indic); reported by `resolvedOptions()` ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **resolved options**: what `resolvedOptions()` returns: the locale, numbering system and options a formatter actually uses after fallback ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **Baseline (MDN)**: MDN's label for browser platform features; newly available means the latest stable Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari support it ([34 §2](../modules/34-i18n.md#2-the-platforms-intl-apis-dates-numbers-plurals-lists-collation-and-display-names))
- **locale data**: the table of month names, patterns, symbols and plural rule that one locale supplies to a formatter; Angular's copy ships in `@angular/common` ([34 §3](../modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- **formatting function**: `formatDate`, `formatNumber`, `formatCurrency` and `formatPercent` from `@angular/common`, which the built-in pipes call ([34 §3](../modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- **`registerLocaleData`**: stores an imported locale array so Angular's formatting functions can find it; it downloads nothing ([34 §3](../modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- **global variant**: a locale file under `@angular/common/locales/global/` that assigns its data to `ng.common.locales` on the global object instead of exporting it ([34 §3](../modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- **Arabic-Indic digits**: the digit characters U+0660 to U+0669 (`٠` to `٩`), used by `Intl` for `ar-EG` where Angular's data prints Latin digits ([34 §3](../modules/34-i18n.md#3-angulars-locale-data-and-formatting-functions-versus-intl))
- **message**: one unit of translatable text: the source text, its placeholders and its metadata; an `i18n` attribute or a `$localize` call makes one ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **placeholder**: a named slot in a message for a part that is not text, such as an interpolation or an element; translators move it but do not change it ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **meaning**: the part of a message's metadata, before `|`, that separates identical texts used differently; it feeds the computed ID ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **description**: the part of a message's metadata, after `|`, that tells the translator the context; it does not affect the ID ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **custom ID**: an ID written after `@@` in a message's metadata that replaces the computed ID ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **tagged template literal**: a template literal written after a function name, which receives the fixed strings and the values separately; `$localize` is one ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **polyfill**: a script the build runs to add a global that code expects, such as `@angular/localize/init` adding `$localize` ([34 §4](../modules/34-i18n.md#4-marking-text-for-translation-i18n-attributes-metadata-ids-and-localize-in-code))
- **ICU message syntax**: the `{variable, plural|select, case {…} …}` notation inside a message, where the translation supplies the branches; not the ICU library ([34 §5](../modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- **plural expression**: an ICU expression that picks a branch by number: an exact match, then the locale's plural category, then `other` ([34 §5](../modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- **select expression**: an ICU expression that picks a branch by the value of a string, else `other` ([34 §5](../modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- **exact match**: an ICU plural case written `=n` that tests the value itself and wins over any plural category ([34 §5](../modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- **nested expression**: an ICU expression written inside a branch of another, such as a plural inside a select ([34 §5](../modules/34-i18n.md#5-icu-expressions-plural-select-and-nesting))
- **extraction**: the build step (`ng extract-i18n`) that collects every message from the compiled app into one source file ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **source file**: the file extraction writes, `messages.xlf` by default, with one entry per message in the source locale ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **XLIFF**: the XML Localization Interchange File Format, the default format of Angular's translation files (version 1.2 by default) ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **trans-unit**: one message in an XLIFF 1.2 file: its `id`, `<source>` text, notes and source location, plus a `<target>` in a translation file ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **target (i18n)**: the translated text a translator adds to a trans-unit; the build inlines it for that locale ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **computed ID**: the ID a message gets when it has no `@@id`: a hash of its text and meaning, so any edit to either changes it ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **legacy ID**: an ID older Angular versions computed for a message; `ng extract-i18n --format legacy-migrate` writes a map from legacy to current IDs ([34 §6](../modules/34-i18n.md#6-extraction-and-translation-files-ng-extract-i18n-formats-and-message-ids))
- **localized build**: an `ng build` with `localize` on: one compilation, then one translated copy of the app per locale ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **inliner**: the build step after compilation that replaces each `$localize` call with its translation and writes one copy per locale ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **`subPath`**: a locale's URL segment in `angular.json`'s `i18n` block; its output folder name and base `href`, the locale code by default ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **base `href`**: the URL in `<base href>` that relative links and the router resolve against; `/fr/` in the French build ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **`lang` attribute**: the language of a page, set on `<html>` per locale by a localized build ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **`dir` attribute**: the text direction of a page, `ltr` or `rtl`, set on `<html>` from the locale data by a localized build ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **`Accept-Language`**: the request header listing the browser's preferred languages, used to pick a locale for `/` ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **`Vary`**: the response header that tells caches which request headers the response depends on ([34 §7](../modules/34-i18n.md#7-compile-time-localization-one-build-per-locale-subpath-lang-and-dir))
- **runtime translation**: keeping `$localize` live in the browser, with the translations loaded before the app starts ([34 §8](../modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- **translation map**: the table from message ID to translated text that `loadTranslations` fills ([34 §8](../modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- **pass-through**: `$localize` without a translation map: it strips the metadata and returns the source text ([34 §8](../modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- **`loadTranslations`**: the `@angular/localize` function that parses translations into the map and installs `$localize.translate` ([34 §8](../modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- **`clearTranslations`**: the `@angular/localize` function that removes `$localize.translate` and empties the map ([34 §8](../modules/34-i18n.md#8-runtime-translation-with-angularlocalize-loadtranslations-before-bootstrap))
- **translation key**: a stable identifier such as `'cart.title'` that a runtime library looks up in the active language's dictionary, instead of the source text ([34 §9](../modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- **loader (translation)**: the class a runtime library calls to get one language's dictionary, or one scope's ([34 §9](../modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- **scope (Transloco)**: a named slice of translations, requested as `<scope>/<lang>` when a component that provides it renders, and merged under its alias ([34 §9](../modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- **child translation service**: an ngx-translate `TranslateService` from `provideChildTranslateService`, with its own loader and store, that asks its parent for keys it lacks ([34 §9](../modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- **fallback language**: the language a runtime library consults when the active one lacks a key ([34 §9](../modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- **missing-key handler**: the class that decides what a runtime library shows for a missing key; both libraries' defaults return the key ([34 §9](../modules/34-i18n.md#9-runtime-translation-libraries-transloco-and-ngx-translate-lazy-scopes-and-the-trade-off-against-built-in-i18n))
- **instant**: one moment in time, the same for everyone; stored as UTC, shown in the user's time zone ([34 §10](../modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- **plain date**: a calendar day with no time, such as a birthday; not an instant ([34 §10](../modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- **UTC**: the zero offset from which time zone offsets and instants are measured ([34 §10](../modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- **ISO 8601**: the international notation for dates and times; JavaScript's date time string format is a simplification of it ([34 §10](../modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- **ISO 4217**: the standard of currency codes such as `EUR` and `JPY`, with each currency's minor unit digits ([34 §10](../modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- **minor unit**: the smallest unit of a currency, such as the cent; `JPY` and `CLP` have 0 digits, `EUR` and `USD` 2 ([34 §10](../modules/34-i18n.md#10-dates-times-numbers-and-money-time-zones-currency-codes-and-minor-units))
- **writing direction**: the way a line of text runs, `ltr` or `rtl` ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **inline axis**: the axis along which lines of text run; `dir` flips it ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **logical property**: a CSS property defined relative to the inline and block axes, such as `margin-inline-start`, so it follows the writing direction ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **physical property**: a CSS property that names a side, such as `margin-left`, and does not follow the writing direction ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **bidi algorithm**: the browser's ordering of text runs of mixed direction, which also places neutral characters such as `!` and spaces ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **isolation (bidi)**: keeping a run of text from affecting how its neighbours are ordered, with `<bdi>` or `dir="auto"` ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **`<bdi>`**: the HTML element that isolates its text from the surrounding text's direction, for text of unknown direction ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **mirroring**: flipping a picture for right-to-left layouts; only direction-bearing icons need it ([34 §11](../modules/34-i18n.md#11-right-to-left-layouts-dir-logical-css-properties-and-bidirectional-text))
- **`consts` function**: the function in a component's compiled definition that builds its template constants, `$localize` messages included; the runtime calls it once per component type ([34 §12](../modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- **opcode (i18n)**: a number or string in the list the runtime parses a message into, encoding steps such as "create this text node" or "switch this ICU" ([34 §12](../modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- **create pass (i18n)**: the first run of a view's template, which builds its nodes and, for a type's first view, parses each message into opcodes ([34 §12](../modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- **update pass (i18n)**: a later run of a view's template, which applies only the update opcodes whose bindings changed ([34 §12](../modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- **ICU case swap**: replacing the nodes of an ICU's active case with those of the case a new value selects ([34 §12](../modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))
- **i18n hydration**: reusing the server-rendered nodes of an `i18n` block in the browser instead of creating them; enabled by `withI18nSupport()` ([34 §12](../modules/34-i18n.md#12-under-the-hood-from-an-i18n-attribute-to-translated-dom))

## Module 35

- **animation**: a change of style values spread over time instead of applied at once ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **CSS transition**: the browser animating a property from its old computed value to its new one when the value changes ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **CSS keyframe animation**: a named `@keyframes` sequence the browser runs while the `animation` property applies to an element ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **keyframe**: the style values at one point of an animation's run ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **Web Animations API**: the platform API through which script creates and controls animations (`element.animate()`, `Animation`, `getAnimations()`) ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **`Animation` object**: the platform's handle on one animation, with a play state and timing, whatever mechanism created it ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **timeline (document)**: the clock that advances every animation of a page ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **enter animation**: an animation that runs when an element is inserted; `animate.enter` adds its classes and removes them when it ends ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **leave animation**: an animation that runs before an element is removed; `animate.leave` delays the DOM removal until it ends ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **Baseline**: MDN's cross-browser label; newly available means support in the latest Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari, widely available at least 2.5 years of it ([35 §1](../modules/35-animations.md#1-motion-in-a-ui-what-to-animate-the-browsers-three-animation-mechanisms-and-where-angular-fits))
- **easing**: the timing function that shapes how fast a transition or animation progresses over its duration ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **transition delay**: the wait between a value change and the start of its transition, set by `transition-delay` ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **reversing (transition)**: running a transition back from the value it has reached when the property changes again mid-way; the running transition is cancelled and a new one starts ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **`@starting-style`**: the at-rule that gives an element the "before" values for its first style update, so an inserted element can transition ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **discrete property**: a property whose values have no in-between steps, such as `display`; it transitions only with `transition-behavior: allow-discrete` ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **`transition-behavior`**: the property whose `allow-discrete` value lets discrete properties transition ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **`CSSTransition`**: the `Animation` subclass the browser creates for each transitioning property; its `transitionProperty` names it ([35 §2](../modules/35-animations.md#2-css-transitions-triggers-interruption-events-and-entry-with-starting-style))
- **`@keyframes`**: the at-rule that declares a named sequence of keyframes an `animation` can run ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **iteration count**: how many times a CSS animation runs, a number or `infinite`, set by `animation-iteration-count` ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **direction (animation)**: whether the iterations of a CSS animation run forward, backward or alternate, set by `animation-direction` ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **play state**: whether a CSS animation is `running` or `paused`, set by `animation-play-state` ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **fill mode (animation)**: what a CSS animation applies outside its active interval: `backwards` the first keyframe during the delay, `forwards` the last keyframe after the end, `both`, or `none` (the default) ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **`CSSAnimation`**: the `Animation` subclass the browser creates for each running CSS animation; its `animationName` names the `@keyframes` rule ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **emulated encapsulation**: Angular's default view encapsulation: the compiler scopes a component's selectors with an attribute and renames its own `@keyframes` ([35 §3](../modules/35-animations.md#3-css-keyframe-animations-keyframes-fill-modes-iteration-and-component-scoped-names))
- **`KeyframeEffect`**: the object that holds an animation's keyframes and timing; an `Animation` plays it ([35 §4](../modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- **`playState`**: an `Animation`'s state: `idle`, `running`, `paused` or `finished` ([35 §4](../modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- **`finished` promise**: the promise of an `Animation` that resolves when it finishes and rejects with an `AbortError` when it is cancelled ([35 §4](../modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- **`commitStyles()`**: the `Animation` method that writes the animation's current values into the element's inline style ([35 §4](../modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- **`playbackRate`**: the speed multiplier of an `Animation`; 1 is normal, 2 twice as fast, 0 stopped ([35 §4](../modules/35-animations.md#4-the-web-animations-api-elementanimate-the-animation-object-and-getanimations))
- **enter classes**: the CSS classes `animate.enter` adds when an element is inserted and removes when its longest animation ends ([35 §5](../modules/35-animations.md#5-animateenter-and-animateleave-with-css-classes-syntax-bindings-and-when-the-element-leaves))
- **leave classes**: the CSS classes `animate.leave` adds before an element is removed; the element leaves the DOM when its longest animation ends ([35 §5](../modules/35-animations.md#5-animateenter-and-animateleave-with-css-classes-syntax-bindings-and-when-the-element-leaves))
- **longest animation**: the animation of an element with the largest delay plus duration, whose end event Angular waits for before removing classes or the element; infinite animations are skipped ([35 §5](../modules/35-animations.md#5-animateenter-and-animateleave-with-css-classes-syntax-bindings-and-when-the-element-leaves))
- **function form (`animate.enter`/`animate.leave`)**: the event-binding syntax `(animate.leave)="fn($event)"` that calls a method with an `AnimationCallbackEvent` instead of adding classes ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **`AnimationCallbackEvent`**: the object a function-form callback receives: `target`, the element, and `animationComplete` ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **`animationComplete`**: the function a leave callback calls to let Angular remove the element; a no-op in an enter callback ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **`MAX_ANIMATION_TIMEOUT`**: the injection token giving the longest wait, in milliseconds, before Angular removes an element whose leave callback never completed (default 4000) ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **injection token**: a typed key under which dependency injection provides a value ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **embedded view**: a view created from a template block (`@if`, `@for`, `@switch`, `ng-template`) inside a component's view ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **component boundary**: the edge of a child component's own view, which nested leave animations do not cross ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **`ViewContainerRef`**: an anchor in a view that holds views and components created at run time ([35 §6](../modules/35-animations.md#6-function-callbacks-and-the-v22-leave-semantics-animationcallbackevent-animationcomplete-and-nested-leaves))
- **reorder (move)**: a change of a row's position in an `@for` that keeps its element; with a stable `track` it starts no leave animation ([35 §7](../modules/35-animations.md#7-animating-lists-for-enter-leave-reorder-and-stagger))
- **stagger**: delaying each item of a list a little more than the one before, to make a cascade; in CSS, an `animation-delay` computed from the item's index ([35 §7](../modules/35-animations.md#7-animating-lists-for-enter-leave-reorder-and-stagger))
- **FLIP**: a pattern, not an Angular feature: First (read positions), Last (let the change happen), Invert (offset each element back with a `transform`), Play (animate the offset to none) ([35 §7](../modules/35-animations.md#7-animating-lists-for-enter-leave-reorder-and-stagger))
- **trigger (legacy)**: a named state machine declared in a component's `animations` array with `trigger()` and bound to an element as `[@name]="expr"` ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **animation state**: a named value of a trigger, with styles given by `state('name', style({…}))`; `void` and `*` are built in ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **transition expression**: the string that says which state change a `transition()` handles, such as `'open <=> closed'` or `':enter'` ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **`void` state**: the state of an element that is not in the DOM; `:enter` is `void => *` and `:leave` is `* => void` ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **wildcard state (`*`)**: matches any state in a transition expression ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **`query` (animation)**: a function that finds descendants of the trigger's element, such as `':enter'`, to animate inside one transition ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **`stagger` (legacy)**: a function that starts each queried element's animation a fixed step after the previous one; the engine builds it into longer keyframe timelines, not a `delay` ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **`animateChild`**: a function that lets a parent transition run the triggers of its child elements ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **`AnimationEvent`**: the object a `(@name.start)` or `(@name.done)` callback receives, with `fromState`, `toState`, `totalTime` and `phaseName` ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **animation driver**: the engine's adapter to the platform: the web driver plays with `element.animate()`, the noop driver plays nothing ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **noop driver**: the animation driver of `provideNoopAnimations()`, which plays nothing and still reports states and callbacks ([35 §8](../modules/35-animations.md#8-legacy-angularanimations-triggers-states-transitions-query-and-stagger))
- **migration path**: the mapping from an old API's features to their replacements and the order in which to apply it ([35 §9](../modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- **wildcard height**: the legacy `height: '*'` style, which animates to the element's natural content height; in CSS, a `grid-template-rows` row or `calc-size()` ([35 §9](../modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- **mixing rule**: legacy `@angular/animations` and `animate.enter`/`animate.leave` must not share a component or a content projection; separate components of one app may use either ([35 §9](../modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- **bundle chunk**: one JavaScript file the builder emits; initial chunks load with the page, lazy chunks on a dynamic `import()` ([35 §9](../modules/35-animations.md#9-migrating-from-angularanimations-to-native-css-and-animateenteranimateleave))
- **view transition**: the browser animating from the old state of the page to the new one, started by `document.startViewTransition(update)` ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **snapshot**: the static image of the page (or of a named element) the browser takes before and after the update ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **pseudo-element tree**: the generated elements `::view-transition`, `::view-transition-group()`, `::view-transition-image-pair()`, `::view-transition-old()` and `::view-transition-new()` that exist only during a view transition ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **`view-transition-name`**: the CSS property that gives an element its own group in the transition; the page itself is the group `root` ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **shared element**: elements on the old and new pages with the same `view-transition-name`, which morph into each other ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **view transition type**: a label on one view transition that CSS can match to pick an animation ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **`ViewTransition`**: the object `startViewTransition` returns, with `ready`, `finished` and `skipTransition()` ([35 §10](../modules/35-animations.md#10-view-transitions-with-the-router-withviewtransitions-view-transition-name-and-onviewtransitioncreated))
- **jank**: motion that stutters because the browser produced a frame late ([35 §11](../modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- **compositor-only property**: a CSS property (`transform`, `opacity`) the browser can change without layout or paint ([35 §11](../modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- **`LayoutCount`**: the DevTools Protocol counter of layout passes the browser has run ([35 §11](../modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- **`prefers-reduced-motion`**: the media query that reports whether the user asked the device for less motion ([35 §11](../modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- **vestibular motion disorder**: a balance-system condition in which on-screen motion can cause discomfort, as MDN describes it ([35 §11](../modules/35-animations.md#11-performance-and-reduced-motion-compositor-properties-measuring-jank-and-prefers-reduced-motion))
- **instruction**: a function call the compiler writes into a component's template function, such as `ɵɵanimateLeave` ([35 §12](../modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- **animation map**: the per-view record (internal `LView[ANIMATIONS]`) of the enter and leave functions of each node, keyed by node index ([35 §12](../modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- **animation queue**: the per-injector set of animation functions (internal `ANIMATION_QUEUE`) that runs once after the next render ([35 §12](../modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- **detach**: the runtime step that takes a view's nodes out of the DOM, which a leave animation postpones ([35 §12](../modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- **fallback timer**: the `setTimeout` of the longest animation's duration plus 50 ms that ends a leave when no end event arrives ([35 §12](../modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))
- **LView**: the internal array holding one template instance's live state ([35 §12](../modules/35-animations.md#12-under-the-hood-from-animateleave-to-a-removed-node))

## Module 36

- **component library**: a package of ready-made UI components; `angular/components` releases three together: Angular Material, the CDK and Angular Aria ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **Angular Material**: `@angular/material`, styled Material Design components for Angular, built on the CDK ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **Material Design 3 (M3)**: the third version of Google's Material Design system, which Angular Material's theming follows ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **Component Dev Kit (CDK)**: `@angular/cdk`, the behavior layer of `angular/components`: overlays, portals, drag and drop, tables, accessibility helpers and test harnesses, with no look ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **behavior primitive**: a service, directive or function that gives UI a behavior (positioning, focus, dragging, keyboard handling) but no markup or styles of its own ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **headless component**: a component or directive that owns behavior and ARIA attributes but renders no styles, so the author supplies markup and look ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **Angular Aria**: `@angular/aria`, headless directives that implement WAI-ARIA patterns on the author's markup; stable since v22.0 ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **WAI-ARIA pattern (APG)**: the W3C ARIA Authoring Practices Guide's description of a widget's keyboard behavior, roles and states, such as the listbox or tabs pattern ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **entry point (secondary entry point)**: an import path of a package, such as `@angular/cdk/dialog`, listed in its `exports` map and imported on its own ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **peer dependency**: a package a library expects the app to install itself, within the range its `peerDependencies` names; Material and Aria name the CDK at an exact version ([36 §1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#1-material-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-styled-components-behavior-primitives-and-headless-patterns))
- **appearance (`matButton`)**: the input of `MatButton` that picks its style: `text`, `filled`, `elevated`, `outlined` or `tonal` ([36 §2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- **dialog ref**: the `MatDialogRef` that `MatDialog.open` returns: a handle to close the dialog or await its result with `afterClosed()` ([36 §2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- **`MatFormFieldControl`**: the contract a control such as `matInput` or `mat-select` implements to sit in a `mat-form-field`; since 22.2 it accepts Signal Forms state ([36 §2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- **`ErrorStateMatcher`**: the Material service whose `isErrorState` decides when a form field shows its error; by default the control is invalid and either touched or in a submitted form ([36 §2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- **`Directionality`**: the CDK service that reports the reading direction, `ltr` or `rtl`, read from the page or from the nearest `dir` directive ([36 §2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- **`MATERIAL_ANIMATIONS`**: the Material injection token `{ animationsDisabled?: boolean }` that switches its motion off; without it Material follows `prefers-reduced-motion` ([36 §2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#2-material-components-in-a-v22-app-standalone-imports-form-fields-dialogs-direction-and-motion))
- **design token**: a named design decision, such as a color role or a type style, stored as a value that code reads by name ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **`mat.theme`**: the Angular Material Sass mixin that turns a palette, a font family and a density into the `--mat-sys-*` system variables at build time ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **system variable (system token)**: a `--mat-sys-*` CSS custom property, such as `--mat-sys-primary`, that `mat.theme` declares and every Material component reads ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **component token**: a Material component's own custom property, such as `--mat-button-filled-container-color`, which falls back to a system variable when unset ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **theme type**: the `mat.theme` color setting `color-scheme` (the default: `light-dark()` values), `light` or `dark` (one set of values) ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **palette (Material)**: the Sass map passed as a theme's color, made of tonal palettes, such as `mat.$azure-palette` ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **tonal palette**: one hue at tones 0 (black) to 100 (white); Material's light theme takes `primary` from tone 40, the dark theme from tone 80 ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **density (Material)**: a spacing scale for components, 0 by default and down to -5 per the theming guide ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **prebuilt theme**: one of the eight compiled CSS theme files in `@angular/material/prebuilt-themes`, each fixed to light or dark ([36 §3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#3-theming-with-material-3-mattheme-system-variables-and-light-and-dark-with-color-scheme))
- **system override**: `mat.theme-overrides((primary: …))` inside a selector, which redefines `--mat-sys-*` variables for that subtree ([36 §4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- **component override**: a component's `*-overrides` mixin, such as `mat.button-overrides((filled-container-color: …))`, which redefines that component's tokens for a subtree ([36 §4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- **override mixin**: a Sass mixin that writes custom properties under the selector that includes it; component ones reject a misspelled token name at build time ([36 §4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- **token name**: the key of an override map, a component token without its `--mat-<component>-` prefix, such as `filled-container-color` ([36 §4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- **overlay pane class**: the class a `panelClass` option puts on an overlay's pane element, where an override for a dialog or menu can sit because they render outside the subtree that opened them ([36 §4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- **strong focus indicator**: an outline drawn on the focused element, switched on once with `mat.strong-focus-indicators()` ([36 §4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#4-customizing-the-theme-system-and-component-token-overrides-and-theming-your-own-components))
- **portal**: content a component, template or DOM node renders somewhere other than where it is written; the CDK has `ComponentPortal`, `TemplatePortal` and `DomPortal` ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **portal outlet**: the place a portal renders into, such as the `CdkPortalOutlet` directive or the pane of an overlay ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **overlay**: an outlet the CDK creates outside your component tree, with a host element, a pane and a position strategy; `createOverlayRef` makes one ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **overlay container**: the `div.cdk-overlay-container` the CDK appends to `body`, where an overlay's host goes by default ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **pane**: the `div.cdk-overlay-pane` inside an overlay's host that carries the overlay's content ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **top layer**: the browser's layer above every stacking context, which holds shown popovers, modal dialogs and fullscreen elements ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **popover (manual)**: an element with `popover="manual"`, which only code shows and hides: it is not light-dismissed, and a shown one is in the top layer ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **position strategy**: the object that places an overlay's pane: global (against the viewport) or flexible connected (against an origin element) ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **origin (overlay)**: the element a connected position strategy places the pane against ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **fallback position**: a later entry in a connected strategy's position list, used when an earlier one does not fit the viewport ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **scroll strategy**: the object that decides what scrolling does while an overlay is open: reposition, block, close or nothing ([36 §5](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#5-portals-and-overlays-where-floating-ui-renders-how-it-is-positioned-and-how-it-closes))
- **drop list**: the `cdkDropList` container that holds drag items, reports each drop and can exchange items with connected lists ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **drag item**: a `cdkDrag` element inside a drop list that the user can pick up ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **drag preview**: the copy of a dragged item that follows the pointer; the CDK appends it to `body` as a manual popover ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **drag placeholder**: the copy of a dragged item left in the list to mark the gap where it will land ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **connected lists**: drop lists that can exchange items, joined with `cdkDropListConnectedTo` or by sitting in one drop list group ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **drop list group**: the `cdkDropListGroup` directive that connects every drop list inside it ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **drag handle**: a `cdkDragHandle` element that limits where a drag may start ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **start threshold**: how far the pointer must move, as horizontal plus vertical distance (5 px by default), before the CDK turns a press into a drag ([36 §6](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#6-drag-and-drop-sorting-transferring-between-lists-and-the-keyboard-gap))
- **column definition**: a `cdkColumnDef` or `matColumnDef` that names a table column and holds its header and cell templates ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **row definition**: a `cdkRowDef` that lists the columns a data row of a CDK table shows ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **header row**: the table row, set by `cdkHeaderRowDef`, that lists the columns whose header templates the table shows ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **DataSource (CDK)**: an object whose `connect` returns an `Observable` of rows for a table and whose `disconnect` cleans up when the table switches source or is destroyed ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **trackBy (table)**: the function that gives a table row an identity across emissions, so equal identities keep the row view ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **MatTableDataSource**: Material's `DataSource` that filters, sorts and pages an array in memory for a `MatTable` ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **sticky column**: a column definition marked `sticky`, which stays in view when the table scrolls sideways ([36 §7](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#7-tables-and-long-lists-cdktable-datasource-mattable-and-virtual-scrolling))
- **step control**: the form control (`stepControl`) a stepper step checks for validity before a linear stepper lets the user leave it ([36 §8](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- **linear stepper**: a stepper marked `linear`, which refuses to leave a step until that step's control is valid, interacted with and not pending ([36 §8](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- **breakpoint**: a viewport size at which the layout changes, written as a media query such as `(max-width: 599.98px)` ([36 §8](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- **secure context**: a window or worker that meets minimum authentication and confidentiality standards, which the async Clipboard API requires ([36 §8](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- **transient user activation**: the window state that a user has recently interacted with it directly, which the browser may require before it copies or pastes ([36 §8](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#8-the-smaller-primitives-the-stepper-breakpointobserver-and-clipboard))
- **focus trap**: a region that sends Tab back inside it, which the CDK builds from two invisible anchor elements around the region ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **focus origin**: how an element received focus, one of `keyboard`, `mouse`, `touch` or `program` (code), which `FocusMonitor` reports ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **input modality**: the kind of input the user produced last (keyboard, mouse or touch), which the CDK's `InputModalityDetector` remembers ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **live announcer**: the CDK's `LiveAnnouncer`, which writes messages into one shared live region element ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **key manager**: a CDK class (`ListKeyManager` and its subclasses) that turns key presses into an active item in a list ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **active item**: the list item a key manager currently points at ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **type-ahead**: choosing a list item by typing the first letters of its label, which a key manager matches after a short pause ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **skip predicate**: the function a key manager calls to decide whether an item is passed over, by default one that skips disabled items ([36 §9](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#9-cdk-accessibility-focustrap-focusmonitor-liveannouncer-and-the-key-managers))
- **harness environment**: the object that connects component harnesses to a test runner; `TestbedHarnessEnvironment` is the TestBed one and `SeleniumWebDriverHarnessEnvironment` the Selenium one ([36 §10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- **harness loader**: an object whose `getHarness`, `getHarnessOrNull` and `getAllHarnesses` find harnesses under a root element ([36 §10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- **host selector**: the static CSS selector (`hostSelector`) of the component a harness wraps ([36 §10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- **locator (harness)**: a function a harness builds with `locatorFor` to find an element or child harness inside its host ([36 §10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- **document root loader**: a harness loader rooted at `document.body`, for content a component renders outside its own element, such as overlays ([36 §10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- **harness predicate (as used in 36)**: a `HarnessPredicate` built by a harness's static `with()`, which narrows a query by options such as a label ([36 §10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#10-component-harnesses-for-material-the-cdk-and-your-own-components))
- **headless directive**: a directive that adds behavior and ARIA state to an element the author wrote and brings no styles, such as Angular Aria's `ngListbox` ([36 §11](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- **pattern (Angular Aria)**: one APG widget that Angular Aria implements as a set of directives: listbox, tabs, menu and menubar, toolbar, accordion, tree, grid or combobox ([36 §11](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- **focus mode**: the Angular Aria input that puts DOM focus on the active item (`roving`) or keeps it on the container, which names the active item in `aria-activedescendant` (`activedescendant`) ([36 §11](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- **selection mode**: the Angular Aria input that makes moving select (`follow`, the default) or leaves selection to Space and Enter (`explicit`) ([36 §11](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- **soft-disabled**: Angular Aria's default for disabled items: they stay focusable but cannot be selected or activated ([36 §11](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- **model input (as used in 36)**: a writable signal input made with `model()`, bound two ways with `[(…)]`; Angular Aria's listbox selection `value` is one ([36 §11](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#11-angular-aria-headless-signal-driven-aria-patterns))
- **design system**: the design tokens, components, usage rules and tests that several products share ([36 §12](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- **wrapper component**: your component that renders another library's component behind your own inputs, such as a design-system button around `matButton` ([36 §12](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- **public API surface (library)**: the inputs, outputs, tokens and exported functions a library's callers depend on ([36 §12](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- **upgrade coupling**: how much of a library's public API surface, or of the code built on it, must change when the library underneath releases ([36 §12](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- **token layering**: exposing your own custom properties whose fallbacks are another system's variables, such as `--mat-sys-*` ([36 §12](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#12-a-design-system-on-the-cdk-and-angular-aria-or-a-wrapper-around-material))
- **structural styles (CDK overlay)**: the CDK's own CSS that places the overlay container, host and pane, with no look, loaded by `createOverlayRef` itself ([36 §13](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- **insertion point (overlay)**: the element a position strategy names for a popover overlay's host, which goes after it or inside it; without one the host goes into the overlay container ([36 §13](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- **dispatcher stack**: the array of attached overlays that the CDK's keyboard and outside-click dispatchers walk from the most recently attached ([36 §13](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- **fallback chain (custom properties)**: nested `var()` calls whose second argument applies when the first custom property is not set, such as a component token falling back to a system variable ([36 §13](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#13-under-the-hood-from-createoverlayref-to-a-popover-in-the-top-layer-and-from-mattheme-to-a-computed-color))
- **theme choice**: a user setting with three values, system, light or dark, where "system" defers to the operating system's `prefers-color-scheme` ([Exercise 36.1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-1))
- **brand override**: a component-token override on an ancestor that fixes one component's color in every color scheme, because the component reads the token before the system variable ([Exercise 36.1](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-1))
- **editable combobox**: a text input with a popup of suggestions, where DOM focus stays in the input and `aria-activedescendant` names the current option ([Exercise 36.2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-2))
- **key relay (Angular Aria combobox)**: `ngCombobox` re-dispatching a copy of a navigation key or Enter on the popup's listbox, on the next render ([Exercise 36.2](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-2))
- **keyboard alternative (drag and drop)**: a control, such as a button or a key chord, that makes the same change as a drag, because the CDK's drag-and-drop listens to pointer events only ([Exercise 36.3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-3))
- **connected drop lists**: drop lists that accept each other's items, through a `cdkDropListGroup` around them or `cdkDropListConnectedTo` naming the others ([Exercise 36.3](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-3))
- **radiogroup**: the ARIA role for a set of mutually exclusive `role="radio"` elements, one checked at a time, which an ARIA rating can use in place of native radio inputs ([Exercise 36.4](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#ex36-4))
- **`ComponentPortal`**: a portal that names a component to create at run time and attach to an outlet ([36 Q36.09](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-09))
- **`TemplatePortal`**: a portal made of a `TemplateRef` and its context, whose view an outlet creates with `createEmbeddedView` ([36 Q36.09](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-09))
- **`DomPortal`**: a portal for an existing DOM element, which the outlet moves into itself (leaving a comment anchor) and puts back on detach ([36 Q36.09](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-09))
- **`usePopover`**: the overlay config option that makes the host a `popover="manual"` element in the top layer; `true` by default, and off where `showPopover` does not exist ([36 Q36.10](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-10))
- **test surface (harness)**: the set of async methods a component harness exposes, which say what a user sees or does and return values rather than elements ([36 Q36.20](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-20))
- **fake clock (Vitest)**: `vi.useFakeTimers` replaces the named timer functions and, when `Date` is listed, the clock too, so code under test sees time pass only when the test runs the timers ([36 Q36.18](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-18))
- **headless component**: a directive or component that carries a widget's behavior, keys and ARIA state but no template or style, so your markup and CSS supply the look ([36 Q36.22](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-22))
- **active descendant (`aria-activedescendant`)**: an ARIA attribute on a focused container that names the id of the option treated as current, so focus stays on the container ([36 Q36.23](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-23))
- **dispatcher stack (overlays)**: the array of attached overlays that the CDK's keyboard and outside-click dispatchers walk from the newest ([36 Q36.25](../modules/36-material-and-cdk.md#q36-25))

## Module 37

- **custom element**: a class the browser runs for a registered tag; Angular Elements builds one from a component ([37 §1](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element))
- **upgrade (custom element)**: the browser turning each existing or later-created tag of a defined name into an instance of its class ([37 §1](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element))
- **Angular Elements**: the `@angular/elements` package, whose `createCustomElement(Component, { injector })` returns a custom element class for one component ([37 §1](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element))
- **environment injector**: the injector that holds an application's providers rather than one component's, which an element's component is created with ([37 §1](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#1-angular-elements-turning-a-component-into-a-custom-element))

- **attribute (versus property)**: text in the markup, as opposed to a value on the element's JavaScript object ([37 §2](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))
- **CustomEvent**: the browser's event class for application events, with its payload in `detail` ([37 §2](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))
- **bubbling**: an event also reaching the listeners of its target's ancestors ([37 §2](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))
- **CUSTOM_ELEMENTS_SCHEMA**: a compiler setting letting a template use dash-named non-Angular elements and properties ([37 §2](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#2-the-elements-public-contract-attributes-properties-events-projected-content-and-typings))

- **destroy delay**: the short wait, 10 ms in the default strategy, between an element's disconnection and its component's destruction ([37 §3](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#3-elements-at-run-time-lifecycle-change-detection-without-zonejs-styles-and-several-apps-on-one-page))
- **ViewEncapsulation.ShadowDom**: component encapsulation that renders the template into a shadow root, so its styles stay inside it ([37 §3](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#3-elements-at-run-time-lifecycle-change-detection-without-zonejs-styles-and-several-apps-on-one-page))

- **service worker**: a script the browser runs apart from the page to handle its requests; Angular's build supplies a ready-made one ([37 §4](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- **registration (service worker)**: the browser's record that a worker script serves a scope ([37 §4](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- **scope (service worker)**: the URLs a registered worker may control ([37 §4](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- **ngsw.json**: the manifest the build generates from `ngsw-config.json`, listing every file with its hash plus `appData` ([37 §4](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- **app version (service worker)**: one `ngsw.json` and the files it names, as installed by the worker ([37 §4](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))
- **appData**: free-form data from `ngsw-config.json` carried inside the generated manifest ([37 §4](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#4-angularservice-worker-registration-ngsw-configjson-and-the-generated-ngswjson))

- **asset group**: a set of the app's own files listed in `ngsw-config.json`, cached and versioned with the app ([37 §5](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- **data group**: a set of API URLs the worker caches with a `performance` or `freshness` strategy, outside the app's versions ([37 §5](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
- **installMode**: an asset group's setting for when its files are downloaded: `prefetch` at install, `lazy` on first request ([37 §5](../modules/37-elements-pwa-errors-ecosystem.md#5-caching-with-asset-and-data-groups-navigation-requests-and-the-app-shell))
